The decoded shipped defaults contain **185 ordered rules and 387 selector occurrences per theme**, with identical selector structure and font settings, and an exact 16-color substitution between Mocha and Latte. These are extracted binary values, not inferred from Catppuccin palette names or upstream plaintext themes.

At Codex `121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df`, the adaptive default selects **Catppuccin Latte** for a detected light background, otherwise **Catppuccin Mocha**, including unavailable background detection. [Selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L194-L235).

The downloaded crate archives matched the SHA-256 checksums in Codex’s lockfile: [two-face 0.5.1](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/Cargo.lock#L16070-L16079), [Syntect 5.3.0](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/Cargo.lock#L14968-L14988), [bincode 1.3.3](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/Cargo.lock#L1408-L1416).

```text
SHA-256
 two-face-0.5.1.crate
 b285c51f8a6ade109ed4566d33ac4fb289fb5d6cf87ed70908a5eaf65e948e34
 syntect-5.3.0.crate
 656b45c05d95a5704399aeef6bd0ddec7b2b3531b7c9e900abbf7c4d2190c925
 bincode-1.3.3.crate
 b1f45e9417d87227c7a56d22e471c6206462cba514c7590c09aff4cf6d1ddcad
 generated/themes.bin - 62,593 bytes
 be28f0c1a167469c5bd1e5311d4d3503e6503b378791f2afb9e3e5f2f7f4642e
```

The archive records two-face upstream commit `61d1e8d42cfe3c80e5f3d1bc9a71a4a59ee9ebee`. `gh api` reads confirmed that its [binary bundle](https://github.com/CosmicHorrorDev/two-face/blob/61d1e8d42cfe3c80e5f3d1bc9a71a4a59ee9ebee/generated/themes.bin), loader, and serialization definitions match the crate byte-for-byte. The inspected Syntect files likewise match its archive-recorded commit `e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27`; bincode source matches its recorded commit `c44b5e364e7084cdbabf9f94b63a3c7f32b8fb68` after CRLF normalization.

The Python standard-library decoder followed these source-defined layers:

1. Uncompressed bincode `BTreeMap<String, LazyTheme>`: **32 entries**. Each `LazyTheme` serializes only its byte vector; the initialized cache is skipped. [Loader](https://github.com/CosmicHorrorDev/two-face/blob/61d1e8d42cfe3c80e5f3d1bc9a71a4a59ee9ebee/src/theme/mod.rs#L34-L39), [map and lazy value](https://github.com/CosmicHorrorDev/two-face/blob/61d1e8d42cfe3c80e5f3d1bc9a71a4a59ee9ebee/src/theme/core_types.rs#L26-L31), [serialization](https://github.com/CosmicHorrorDev/two-face/blob/61d1e8d42cfe3c80e5f3d1bc9a71a4a59ee9ebee/src/theme/core_types.rs#L103-L129).
2. Each selected vector is a **zlib-wrapped DEFLATE stream**, inflated into bincode `Theme`. Both inflate to **28,420 bytes**. [Compression/deserialization](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/dumps.rs#L119-L145).
3. Bincode uses little-endian fixed-width integers: sequence/string lengths are `u64`, option tags one byte, colors four RGBA bytes, font bits one byte. `Scope` serializes as a string, not its internal packed integers. Struct fields occur in declared order. [Bincode configuration](https://github.com/servo/bincode/blob/c44b5e364e7084cdbabf9f94b63a3c7f32b8fb68/src/lib.rs#L90-L136), [endianness](https://github.com/servo/bincode/blob/c44b5e364e7084cdbabf9f94b63a3c7f32b8fb68/src/config/mod.rs#L98-L102), [lengths](https://github.com/servo/bincode/blob/c44b5e364e7084cdbabf9f94b63a3c7f32b8fb68/src/config/int.rs#L119-L125), [options/strings](https://github.com/servo/bincode/blob/c44b5e364e7084cdbabf9f94b63a3c7f32b8fb68/src/ser/mod.rs#L121-L153), [theme fields](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/highlighting/theme.rs#L10-L127), [selector fields](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/highlighting/selector.rs#L13-L25), [scope serialization](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/parsing/scope.rs#L356-L389), [colors/fonts](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/highlighting/style.rs#L22-L80).

| Theme | Compressed byte interval in `themes.bin`, zero-based/end-exclusive | Inflated SHA-256                                                   |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Latte | `[5382,9709)`                                                      | `4c7dddaae66120875b5cbd367239379bb2964f09c932bccd28c05d10bce7cc2b` |
| Mocha | `[14114,18446)`                                                    | `d0726566e7bd8e39a93de04533b47cc1e6c91dcb6107b812f7da9db09d8fdf96` |

Extraction consumed the entire outer bundle and both inflated themes, checked zlib completion without trailing data, and independently re-encoded both decoded objects to **identical inflated bytes**. The research artifacts at `../codex-tool-display-revision/syntax-theme-data/` contained every rule's byte interval and the decoder/round-trip checker, but are not included with this reference. The recorded hashes and decoded tables are retained below; the extraction cannot be rerun from this reference alone. This is static data extraction, not execution of Codex or the syntax highlighter.

#### Complete shared scope-to-style table

This table reproduces **all 185 serialized rules in order**, including repeated selectors and the literal spelling `constant.charactger.entity.js.jsx` in rule 68. Commas separate alternatives within one rule; spaces inside a selector separate scopes in its matching path. All exclusion vectors and saved/cleared scope stacks are empty. Identical selector structure and color substitution were checked directly between both decoded themes.

**Every rule’s background is `None`.** Foreground `-` means `None`. Font `-` means `None` (do not modify that field); `0` means `Some(empty)` (explicitly clear font flags); `B` means `Some(BOLD=1)`; `I` means `Some(ITALIC=4)`. No rule supplies UNDERLINE. These are stored modifiers, **not independently resolved token styles**. The table’s authority is the checksum-verified [shipped binary](https://github.com/CosmicHorrorDev/two-face/blob/61d1e8d42cfe3c80e5f3d1bc9a71a4a59ee9ebee/generated/themes.bin), decoded using the cited types above.

#### Theme data versus highlighter application

Syntect starts with the theme’s global foreground/background and empty font flags. It matches the grammar-produced **scope stack**, scoring foreground, background, and the complete font-bit field separately; a stronger matching rule can replace a field. An unset field does not erase an earlier value. This is not a last-row-wins stylesheet: updates require a strictly greater score. [Initial style and matching](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/highlighting/highlighter.rs#L237-L339).

Specificity scores use **f64**, independently of Codex's f32 color arithmetic. Single-scope selectors are stably sorted by descending atom count. On a scope-stack push at zero-based depth `i`, an atom-prefix match scores `selector_atom_count * 2^(3*i)`; each field starts at score −1 and updates only on a strictly greater score. Multiple-scope selectors are applied afterward in stored order. Their path greedily matches atom-prefix scopes while scanning the stack from oldest to newest, adding `matched_atom_count * 2^(3*i)` at each match and returning on the first complete path. Empty selectors score 1; any matching exclusion rejects a selector, although these shipped themes have no exclusions. [Single/multiple selector application](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/highlighting/highlighter.rs#L237-L339), [path score](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/parsing/scope.rs#L550-L568), [selector/exclusion rules](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/highlighting/selector.rs#L34-L56).

For example, rule 27 stores BOLD for `keyword.operator.new`, but rule 7 already supplies an explicit empty font value for that identical selector. The equal-score later font update does not win. The table must therefore not be converted into a dictionary that silently overwrites duplicate selectors. Exact source-token results additionally depend on the selected grammar and surrounding scopes; this extraction does not execute the grammar/highlighter.

Codex’s ordinary syntax conversion preserves the resolved RGB foreground, **discards background**, and retains **only BOLD**, dropping ITALIC and UNDERLINE without color-depth quantization. Thus the shipped Mocha `comment` rule supplies **`#9399b2` + ITALIC** to Syntect, while Codex drops that italic modifier. The theme’s `selection` alpha values above are editor-setting data, not opacity applied to ordinary code spans. [Converter](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L496-L535).

Rules 53/54 color inserted/deleted diff text, but provide **no diff background**. Since every scope background in these defaults is unset, Codex’s separate diff-background lookup obtains `None` for both inserted and deleted, allowing its hardcoded diff palette fallback; it does not substitute the theme’s global background. [Lookup](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L274-L315). Other UI colors and family-specific diff composition remain outside this data table.

## Lifecycle and ownership

### Inputs and presentation state

The inspected Rust TUI consumes app-server `ServerNotification` values and typed `ThreadItem`s. Do not build an adapter around the older core `ExecCommandBegin`/`End` event names simply because some test names still use them. Tool identity selects a family renderer; there is no universal card state machine or enclosing border/background in `HistoryCell`. [Dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol.rs#L76-L136), [cell contract](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L176-L285).

| Protocol input                | Fields that determine the display                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CommandExecution`            | `id`, `command`, `source`, `command_actions`, `aggregated_output`, `exit_code`, `duration_ms`; `process_id` also routes background-terminal activity. The payload includes `cwd`, but `ExecCell` does not render a generic cwd field. |
| `FileChange`                  | `id`, vector of `{path, kind, diff}`, status; converted to a path-keyed display map with the chat widget's cwd.                                                                                                                       |
| `McpToolCall`                 | `id`, `server`, `tool`, `arguments`, status, optional result/error, duration; result rendering is content-type-specific.                                                                                                              |
| `CommandExecutionOutputDelta` | `item_id` and text delta; match the call ID, not the last visible row.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `TurnPlanUpdated`             | Optional explanation and steps with pending/in-progress/completed statuses; independent of proposed-plan Markdown.                                                                                                                    |
| `TerminalInteraction`         | Process ID and stdin; empty stdin means a background-output poll.                                                                                                                                                                     |

The enum spellings above are Rust identifiers; wire enum values and field names are camelCase. Command status is `InProgress | Completed | Failed | Declined`; source is `Agent | UserShell | UnifiedExecStartup | UnifiedExecInteraction` (default `Agent`). Patch status has the same four values; MCP has only `InProgress | Completed | Failed`. There is no command-level `Cancelled` enum at this boundary. [Item payloads](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/app-server-protocol/src/protocol/v2/item.rs#L289-L354), [enums](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/app-server-protocol/src/protocol/v2/item.rs#L1058-L1193).

```text
item start / output delta / item completion
  → ChatWidget: route by family and call ID; queue behind answer streaming
  → primary active_cell: Option<Box<dyn HistoryCell>>
      ExecCell: ordered calls, potentially several concurrent explorations
      MCP / web search: their own mutable cell
  → flush: take active cell → AppEvent::InsertHistoryCell
  → App: Arc<dyn HistoryCell> retained in transcript_cells
      ├─ rich/raw display → native terminal scrollback
      └─ transcript projection → Ctrl+T pager

Ctrl+T also appends a render-only live tail. It does not commit that tail.
```

There is one **primary mutable history-cell slot**, not one slot per running tool. `running_commands`, unified-exec process summaries, transient hooks, and asynchronous usage/status cells have separate ownership. A completed exploration can remain in the active slot to absorb another compatible call. Conversely, an MCP result can insert an extra image notice. Never infer execution serialization or a one-call/one-row invariant from this layout. [Transcript state](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/transcript.rs#L34-L101), [running-process state](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/exec_state.rs#L8-L19), [composition](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/rendering.rs#L133-L201).

### Start, delta, completion: exact routing

1. **Start:** flush pending answer text first. For unified-exec startup, track the process even if no tool cell will be drawn. Unified-exec start returns without materializing a cell when no task is running or when parsed actions are empty/contain `Unknown`; ensure the working indicator before the latter return. Ordinary agent/user-shell starts do not use that eligibility gate. [Start gate](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L19-L52), [standard-call predicate](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/exec_state.rs#L57-L83).
2. **Ordering:** `defer_or_handle` queues start/end payloads whenever an answer stream exists **or the interrupt queue is already nonempty**. Keeping later events queued preserves FIFO; a completion cannot overtake its queued start. Output deltas use their direct handler, not this FIFO. [Queue](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/streaming.rs#L444-L475).
3. **Immediate start handler:** record command/actions/source in `running_commands[id]`, annotate skill reads, suppress duplicate unified interaction commands, then try `active ExecCell.add_call(...)`. Failure to join flushes the old cell and creates a new one. An ordinary command cannot join an exploration merely because it uses the same shell tool. [Start handler](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L242-L314).
4. **Delta:** update unified-exec recent-output bookkeeping first; if the task is stopped, return. Only a matching call in the primary active `ExecCell` receives the delta. Successful append bumps revision and requests redraw. There is no scan of committed history to append late output. [Delta](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L54-L74).
5. **Completion:** normalize final payload, remove running metadata, discard a suppressed call's completion, and choose the target below. Tracked begin metadata takes precedence over completion metadata for command/actions/source. Unified interaction completion deliberately stores empty output instead of aggregated stdout/stderr. [Completion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L321-L457).

| Active slot at completion                                | Target and mutation                                                                                                                                             |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Exec containing the call ID                              | Complete the **most recent matching ID**. Replace output, assign duration, clear start time; flush according to `should_flush`, otherwise bump revision/redraw. |
| Exec with unfinished calls, but not this ID              | Create a separate completed history cell via direct `InsertHistoryCell`. Preserve the active group.                                                             |
| MCP cell, regardless of matching exec metadata           | Same separate-history route; do not consume the MCP cell.                                                                                                       |
| Finished unrelated exec, another cell family, or no cell | Flush previous active cell, reconstruct this command from final data, then commit it or retain it as a completed exploration group.                             |

A suppressed unified-interaction start still entered `running_commands`; its ID goes into `suppressed_exec_calls`, and completion removes both records without rendering. Duplicate detection compares the displayed joined command string against the previous unified-interaction command; a non-interaction start resets the streak. [Suppression and routing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L263-L457).

**Completion normalization:**

```text
duration = milliseconds(max(duration_ms ?? 0, 0))
if status == Completed:
    exit = exit_code ?? 0
else:
    exit = (exit_code is present and nonzero) ? exit_code : 1
output = aggregated_output ?? ""
```

Thus `Completed + exit 7` still renders failure; `Failed + exit 0` and `Declined + missing exit` become exit 1. Status is not mapped to a universally colored badge. Exec and exploration interpret the normalized result differently. [Normalization](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L337-L359).

### Completion is distinct from commit and interruption

`ExecCell.is_active()` means **any call has no duration**, not “there is no output.” Live deltas can create an output object before completion. `should_flush()` is:

```text
if any call has output.exit_code != 0:
    return no calls remain active
return not exploration AND every call has duration
```

Consequences: successful exploration remains joinable; failed exploration waits for overlapping siblings, then flushes; ordinary commands flush when finished. A new visible history cell normally breaks grouping, except while an assistant stream tail owns the slot. A transcript-only cell breaks a **completed** exec group, not a still-running one. Starting assistant text flushes the wait streak and active group. [Model](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L115-L173), [commit boundaries](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1199-L1260), [assistant boundary](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L8-L24).

Interruption/error termination calls `finalize_turn`: discard preview-only assistant tail; take the active cell; call `mark_failed` only for exec/MCP; insert the cell; clear running/suppressed command tracking and turn-scoped transient hooks. For exec, each unfinished call receives exit 1 and elapsed duration while retaining already collected output; completed calls stay unchanged. Exploration still renders the dim `Explored` summary, so the source comment promising a red `✗` is **not** a universal visible result. Other cell families are not automatically converted into failed exec/MCP cells. A separate interruption notice may follow; it is not a cancellation label on every tool row. [Finalization](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/turn_runtime.rs#L320-L353), [active failure dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1390-L1403), [exec failure mutation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L145-L160), [interruption notice](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/input_restore.rs#L235-L265).

### Rich, transcript, and raw are separate projections

| `HistoryCell` member                           | Exact default contract                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `display_lines(width)`                         | Required: logical Ratatui `Line`/`Span` rows for compact rich chat.                                                      |
| `raw_lines()`                                  | Required: family-specific plain logical lines, not original protocol bytes.                                              |
| `display_hyperlink_lines(width)`               | Defaults to display lines with no extra hyperlink metadata.                                                              |
| `display_lines_for_mode(width, mode)`          | Rich: visible hyperlink lines. Raw: `raw_lines()`; width can be ignored by that family.                                  |
| `transcript_lines(width)`                      | Defaults to `display_lines(width)`; more detail requires an explicit override.                                           |
| `transcript_hyperlink_lines(width)`            | Defaults to plain metadata around transcript lines, not automatically the display hyperlinks.                            |
| `desired_height` / `desired_transcript_height` | `Paragraph.line_count(width)` with `Wrap { trim: false }`, then convert to u16, falling back to 0 on conversion failure. |
| `has_stable_transcript_height()`               | `true`; externally backed cells can opt out.                                                                             |
| `is_stream_continuation()`                     | `false`.                                                                                                                 |
| `transcript_animation_tick()`                  | `None`; animated transcript content must opt in.                                                                         |

The trait is `Debug + Send + Sync + Any`; downcasts route lifecycle updates to concrete cell types. A logical line may exceed the available columns - for example, a URL intentionally retained intact by adaptive wrapping. Physical row measurement must include that overflow. Pi's `Component.render(width)` has a different width contract, detailed in the integration chapter. [Full trait](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L186-L285).

### Raw-output switching and transcript framing

`/raw` toggles raw scrollback; `/raw on` and `/raw off` set it explicitly, with trimmed, ASCII-case-insensitive arguments. Invalid arguments emit `"Usage: /raw [on|off]"`. The default global raw-toggle binding is **Alt+R**, configurable through `tui.keymap.global.toggle_raw_output`. Keyboard toggling is silent; slash commands insert the info message `"Raw output mode on: transcript text is shown for clean terminal selection."` or `"Raw output mode off: rich transcript rendering restored."`. Switching updates active answer/plan stream controllers and immediately reflows retained history; redraw failure inserts `"Failed to redraw transcript: {err}"`. This changes the main scrollback projection, not each cell's separate transcript method. [Slash toggle](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/slash_dispatch.rs#L437-L440), [arguments](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/slash_dispatch.rs#L782-L792), [usage literal](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/slash_dispatch.rs#L39-L39), [binding/default](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/keymap.rs#L1491-L1497), [state/notices](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1600-L1639), [reflow](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/input.rs#L210-L237).

Raw scrollback uses `HistoryLineWrapPolicy::Terminal`: preserve the cell's logical lines and let the terminal soft-wrap them. Rich scrollback uses `PreWrap`: adaptively wrap with the original leading-whitespace continuation prefix, except a URL-only logical line stays intact. This is a second wrapping stage after the family renderer; raw mode does not undo family-level formatting or truncation. [Policy selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/resize_reflow.rs#L228-L234), [wrapping stage](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/insert_history.rs#L230-L265).

**Ctrl+T** opens the transcript, initially following the bottom. Its generic pager clears the surface, renders a dim `"/ T R A N S C R I P T"` over dim repetitions of `"/ "`, and places unstyled `"~"` at the start of unused content rows. A dim `"─"` rule spans the bottom. The transcript reserves three rows below the pager, uses the first two for hints, and leaves the third blank; the pager itself reserves one header and one rule row. Thus normal content height is `max(height−5,0)`. [Initialization](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L503-L520), [framing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L193-L291), [content inset](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L349-L355), [transcript allocation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L898-L909).

When all history is known, overlay the rule with dim `" {percent}% "`: 100 for empty/fitting content, otherwise round the f32 expression `min(scroll_offset,max_scroll)/max_scroll*100`, where `max_scroll=max(total_rows−content_height,0)`. Its x-coordinate is literally `rule.x + rule.width − text_width − 1`, without saturating subtraction; extremely narrow widths are not protected by this calculation. Hide the percentage while loading, partial, or failed history is present. Those states instead overwrite the header's right end with the following dim literal, clipping to the viewport; the complete-state label also appears while its percentage remains available. [Percentage](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L270-L290), [visibility predicate](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L440-L459), [labels](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L911-L932).

| Transcript history state        | Literal header suffix                      |
| ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| Idle                            | None                                       |
| LoadingOlder / LoadingBeginning | `" loading older history... "`             |
| Partial                         | `" partial history \| PgUp for earlier "`  |
| Failed                          | `" history unavailable \| PgUp to retry "` |
| Complete                        | `" start of history "`                     |

Hint lines are dim, begin with one space, separate actions with three spaces, join alternative keys with `"/"`, and insert one space before each description. First line describes `"to scroll"`, `"to page"`, and `"to jump"` using primary configured up/down, page-up/down, and top/bottom hints. Second line starts with the primary close key and `"close"`; normally it adds hardcoded Esc `"to edit prev"`. With a selected user message, it instead adds Esc/Left `"to edit prev"`, Right `"to edit next"`, and Enter `"to edit message"`. These are prompt-edit hints, not actions to rerun a tool. Defaults accept Up/`k`, Down/`j`; PgUp/Shift+Space/Ctrl+B, PgDown/Space/Ctrl+F; Ctrl+U/Ctrl+D half pages; Home/End; `q`/Ctrl+C or Ctrl+T to close. Page movement uses the last rendered content height; ordinary scroll changes by one row. [Hint assembly](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L108-L150), [transcript hints](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L870-L895), [navigation arithmetic](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L293-L346), [bindings](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/keymap.rs#L1706-L1725).

### Whitespace, paint, cache, and replay

- **Committed history:** convert the boxed cell to `Arc`, retain it in `App.transcript_cells`, update an open transcript overlay, then emit rich/raw display rows to scrollback. Before a nonempty, non-stream-continuation cell, insert exactly one empty logical line if history has already emitted such content. No initial blank line for the first history entry. An empty cell emits no separator. While any overlay is open, defer terminal history writes. [Insertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/history_ui.rs#L24-L73), [separator/defer policy](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/resize_reflow.rs#L87-L123).
- **Live layout:** primary active cell receives a one-row top inset, except the initial session header (zero). Its transcript region flexes; the bottom pane has flex 0 and a one-row top inset. Optional ambient-pet reserve reduces available width. There is no automatic full-width tool border. [Composition](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/rendering.rs#L133-L201).
- **Paint:** clear the active draw area before rendering; bottom-anchor overflow by scrolling `max(actual_rows − viewport_height, 0)`. This prevents stale glyphs after shrinking content or resizing. [Paint](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L288-L313).
- **Active layout cache:** key is cell identity, revision, width, render mode, syntax-theme revision. Cache semantic desired height separately from actual rendered height. Bumping revision uses wrapping u64 addition and clears the cache; taking the active cell clears it before address reuse. [Cache](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/transcript.rs#L8-L101).
- **Transcript live tail:** key is width + active revision + stream-continuation flag + optional animation tick. Identical key: do nothing. Changed key: remove old live-tail renderable and compute a replacement. No live key: drop the tail. Preserve a user-scrolled position; follow bottom only if already there. The tail can also include asynchronous usage/status output and is never a second committed tool cell. [Tail source](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1905-L1958), [pager](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L756-L803).
- **Replay:** reconstruct semantic cells from turn items. In-progress exec/MCP calls use starts; completed/failed agent and unified-startup commands use the immediate completion handler during replay; other command cases use normal completion routing. A replayed web-search item is synthesized through begin/end. In-progress file-change items are ignored by the shared item handler. Dynamic-tool and sleep items emit no generic history cell. Historical replay avoids live-only side effects; it is not a recording of old terminal pixels. [Replay dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L185-L289).

Automatic approval review uses a separate global status, keyed by review ID. One pending review shows `"Reviewing approval request"` with its detail and a one-line detail budget. Multiple reviews show `"Reviewing N approval requests"`, the first three details as `"• {detail}"`, then `"+R more"` if needed, with a four-line detail budget. Zero reviews produce no guardian status. Updating an existing ID preserves its position. This uses the status rendering described under background terminals; it does not add a pending badge to the tool cell. [Review status](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/status_state.rs#L50-L108).

`McpToolCallProgress` and `FileChangePatchUpdated` notifications are explicitly ignored in this dispatcher. Do not infer a generic progress meter or live patch-refresh channel from their protocol existence. [Ignored notifications](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol.rs#L239-L258).

## Exec and exploration

This chapter specifies observed Codex behavior at commit `121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df`. Quoted strings use JSON-style escapes; every space inside quotes is significant. `default` means no explicit foreground/background/modifier, not white or an explicit color reset. Recommendations for Pi are not part of this specification.

### Local model and lifecycle

`ExecCell` stores ordered `calls` and `animations_enabled`. Each `ExecCall` retains `call_id`, original `command: Vec<String>`, `parsed`, optional `output`, `source`, optional `start_time`, optional `duration`, and optional `interaction_input`. `CommandOutput` contains `exit_code`, finalized interleaved stdout/stderr `aggregated_output`, and optional `live_output`; its default is exit code zero, empty aggregate, no live buffer. Rendering prefers the live buffer whenever present. There are no separate stdout/stderr rendering channels. [Model and output accessors](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L17-L79)

| Local operation/state                 | Exact effect                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| New active command                    | One call; `output=None`, `duration=None`, `start_time=now`; retain source/input.                                                                                                                                             |
| `add_call`                            | Construct the same pending state; append only when both existing cell and incoming call qualify as exploration. No completion, failure, size, or duplicate-ID gate. Otherwise return `false`.                                |
| `append_output(id, chunk)`            | Empty chunk or unmatched ID: `false`. Otherwise select the **last** matching call, create default output/live buffer if absent, append, return `true`. Does not modify timing.                                               |
| `complete_call(id, output, duration)` | Last matching call: replace the entire output object, set duration, clear start time, return `true`; unmatched: `false`, no mutation.                                                                                        |
| `mark_failed()`                       | For each call with no duration, record elapsed start time or zero, clear start time, create output if necessary, set exit code to **1**. Preserve existing output, including live preview. Completed calls remain untouched. |
| Snapshot/frozen state                 | `freeze_snapshot()` only disables animations. It does not complete, fail, or copy calls.                                                                                                                                     |

Sources: [construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L44-L65), [append/completion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L89-L133), [failure/freeze/deltas](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L147-L203).

Activity means **any duration is absent**, not “output absent.” `active_start_time()` takes the first unfinished call's start time, even if that is `None`; it does not search onward for a timestamp. There is no local abort or replay enum. Abort represented by `mark_failed` renders as ordinary exit-1 failure; replayed calls render from their supplied fields. Neither append nor completion rejects already completed calls: a late delta can create a new live buffer that hides an existing aggregate without reactivating the call. [State predicates and mutation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L120-L203)

| Any unfinished call? | Any stored nonzero exit? | Exploration? | `should_flush()` |
| -------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------ | ---------------- |
| Yes                  | Either                   | Either       | `false`          |
| No                   | Yes                      | Either       | `true`           |
| No                   | No                       | Yes          | `false`          |
| No                   | No                       | No           | `true`           |

This is a predicate, not an insertion operation. Successful exploration can continue accumulating after completion. [Flush implementation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L135-L145)

### Exploration classification and read coalescing

A call qualifies exactly when `source != UserShell`, `parsed` is nonempty, and every parsed item is `Read`, `ListFiles`, or `Search`. All calls must qualify for the cell to qualify. `UnifiedExecInteraction` is not explicitly excluded. Empty metadata or one `Unknown` selects ordinary-command rendering, which requires **exactly one call** and otherwise panics. The renderer consumes supplied metadata; it does not infer exploration from executable names. The upstream parser separately removes consecutive equal parsed commands, then replaces the entire result with one `Unknown` if any unknown remains. [Eligibility](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L163-L169) · [call classification](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L205-L227) · [dispatch/assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L187-L193) [and](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L352-L355) · [parser postprocessing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/shell-command/src/parse_command.rs#L54-L84)

Walk calls in order. If a call contains only reads, consume the maximal consecutive run of **whole read-only calls**, flatten their items, deduplicate `Read.name` by exact string equality in first-seen order, and emit one row. Otherwise process that call's items individually. Thus adjacent reads inside a mixed search/read call remain separate; deduplication is by display name, not path. Grouping does not alter stored calls. [Grouping algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L275-L333)

| Item                           | Label      | Payload spans, in order                                                  |
| ------------------------------ | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Read-only run                  | `"Read"`   | Unique names separated by `", "`.                                        |
| Read in mixed call             | `"Read"`   | `name`; ignore `cmd` and `path`.                                         |
| ListFiles                      | `"List"`   | `path` if `Some`, even empty; otherwise `cmd`.                           |
| Search: query and path present | `"Search"` | query, `" in "`, path.                                                   |
| Search: query only             | `"Search"` | query.                                                                   |
| Search: no query               | `"Search"` | `cmd`, even if path exists.                                              |
| Unknown                        | `"Run"`    | `cmd`; defensive branch unreachable through normal exploration dispatch. |

[Item formatting](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L296-L333)

The header is marker + `" "` + `"Exploring"` while active, otherwise dim `"•"` + `" "` + `"Explored"`. No output, exit code, duration, error badge, or per-call activity marker is rendered - even for failed reads. Each payload wraps with label + `" "` as initial indent and label-width spaces thereafter (`Read`/`List`: 5; `Search`: 7). This wrapping uses the **entire supplied width**, then adds outer `"  └ "` to the first row of the entire group and `"    "` to every later row. It does not subtract those four outer columns first or impose a row limit on the returned lines. The shared desired-height conversion still reports 0 if the measured viewport height exceeds `u16::MAX`; see “Transcript and raw projections” below. [Exploration layout](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L255-L350)

### Command normalization, headers, and stdin interaction

Ordinary commands and transcript commands use `strip_bash_lc_and_escape`: return a recognized shell script verbatim; otherwise `shlex::try_join(argv)`, falling back to `argv.join(" ")` on failure. Despite its name, it also unwraps PowerShell. No whitespace trimming, newline flattening, or script-prefix removal occurs here. The separate `split_command_string` accepts a shlex split if rejoining matches the original exactly, or if the original lacks `":\\"` and re-splitting the join reproduces the tokens; otherwise it preserves the whole string as one argument. [Normalization and assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_command.rs#L8-L106)

Shell extraction rules:

- Bash/Zsh/Sh: exactly `[shell, "-lc" or "-c", script]`; shell detection matches lowercase names directly or recursively uses the platform path's `file_stem`, permitting recognized paths/`.exe` forms. No additional arguments accepted.
- PowerShell: at least three arguments, detected PowerShell executable; scan case-insensitive flags `-nologo`, `-noprofile`, `-command`, `-c`. Unknown flags before the command flag reject extraction. Return the argument immediately after the first command flag; later arguments are ignored.

[Shell helper composition](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/shell-command/src/parse_command.rs#L39-L42) · [POSIX wrapper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/shell-command/src/bash.rs#L106-L119) · [PowerShell wrapper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/shell-command/src/powershell.rs#L9-L71) · [shell detection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/shell-command/src/shell_detect.rs#L39-L59)

For an ordinary single-command cell, status requires **both duration and output**:

| Duration | Output                      | Marker           | Non-interaction title                         |
| -------- | --------------------------- | ---------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| Absent   | Absent or present, any exit | Activity marker  | `"Running"`, including user-shell calls.      |
| Present  | Present, exit 0             | Green bold `"•"` | UserShell: `"You ran"`; otherwise `"Ran"`.    |
| Present  | Present, nonzero exit       | Red bold `"•"`   | Same completed titles.                        |
| Present  | Absent                      | Activity marker  | Same completed titles; no result body/footer. |

Header composition is marker + `" "` + bold title + `" "` + highlighted command. Compact view never appends exit-code text or duration. [Header construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L352-L386)

An `ExecCell` interaction uses the same status marker but no title: marker + `" "` + highlighted summary. Command display unwraps **only** the Bash/Zsh/Sh helper, otherwise joins argv with single spaces without shell escaping. Summary literals are:

```text
"Interacted with `{command}`, sent `{preview}`"   # nonempty Some(input)
"Waited for `{command}`"                        # None or empty input
```

Preview replaces LF with literal `"\\n"`, then backticks with ``"\\`"``; count Unicode scalar values **after** replacement. Keep at most 80 scalars, appending ASCII `"..."` only when longer. This can split an escape pair, not UTF-8. CR, tabs, and command backticks/newlines receive no corresponding sanitization. The entire summary goes through Bash highlighting; backticks are literal text, not a Markdown code-span renderer. Nonempty interaction output appears compactly; only its empty-output placeholder is suppressed. Its transcript suppresses **all** output, but still shows its normalized command and completion footer. [Interaction strings](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L33-L35) [and algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L67-L96) · [compact processing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L382-L490) · [transcript exception](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L211-L235)

### Span styles and activity marker

All locally constructed spans have default background. Only ANSI-decoded output can retain an explicit background. No cell-wide background, border, automatic error-body red, or filename underline is added.

| Span                                                              | Foreground                         | Modifiers                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| `Exploring`, `Explored`, `Running`, `Ran`, `You ran`              | default                            | BOLD                                          |
| Header separating `" "`                                           | default                            | none                                          |
| Completed ordinary `"•"`                                          | Green / Red by success             | BOLD                                          |
| Completed exploration `"•"`                                       | default                            | DIM, regardless of failure                    |
| Exploration labels                                                | Cyan                               | none                                          |
| Exploration names/query/path/command                              | default                            | none                                          |
| `", "`, `" in "`                                                  | default                            | DIM                                           |
| Exploration first `"  └ "`                                        | default                            | DIM                                           |
| Exploration later outer `"    "`, label-space/continuation-indent | default                            | none                                          |
| Command continuations `"  │ "`, including command ellipsis gutter | default                            | DIM                                           |
| Compact output first `"  └ "`                                     | default                            | DIM                                           |
| Compact output subsequent `"    "`                                | default                            | none; replacement ellipsis prefix is DIM      |
| `"(no output)"`, both ellipsis message types                      | default                            | DIM                                           |
| Compact output text                                               | ANSI-decoded foreground/background | Preserve decoded style, add DIM to every span |
| Transcript `"$ "`                                                 | Magenta                            | none                                          |
| Transcript command continuation `"    "`                          | default                            | none                                          |
| Transcript output                                                 | ANSI-decoded foreground/background | Decoded modifiers only; no forced DIM         |
| Transcript `"✓"` / `"✗"`                                          | Green / Red                        | BOLD                                          |
| Transcript failure `" ({exit_code})"`                             | default                            | none                                          |
| Transcript suffix `" • {duration}"`                               | default                            | DIM                                           |

The table follows span construction, not snapshot appearance or guaranteed terminal emission. The shared style chapter documents the custom writer’s HIDDEN omission and `BOLD | DIM → BOLD` intensity-reset behavior; the locked dependency subsection **Delegated color detection and ANSI parsing** specifies decoded ANSI styles. [Output styling](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L128-L170) · [transcript styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L195-L235) · [exploration styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L255-L350) · [command/output gutters](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L352-L490) · [ellipsis styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L621-L647)

Command/interaction text uses Syntect Bash scopes with the active theme: foreground plus BOLD only; theme backgrounds, italics, and underlines are discarded. Alpha 0 maps the red channel to ANSI palette index (0–7: Black, Red, Green, Yellow, Blue, Magenta, Cyan, Gray; 8–255: Indexed); alpha 1 leaves foreground unset; other alpha values use RGB. Default theme is Catppuccin Latte for detected light background, Mocha otherwise; light means `0.299R + 0.587G + 0.114B > 128`. Explicit themes can override this. Therefore command tokens have no single fixed semantic palette. Highlighting falls back to default-style source lines above 524,288 bytes, 10,000 lines, or 4,096 bytes in any line, or on lookup/parsing failure. Empty input yields one empty line; a terminal newline adds no phantom line. Highlighted fragments trim trailing CR/LF characters; fallback uses Rust `str::lines()`. [Theme selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L194-L235) · [lightness](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/color.rs#L1-L5) · [style conversion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L468-L533) · [limits/fallback](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L583-L702)

Activity is static default-foreground DIM `"•"` when animations are disabled. Animated non-truecolor output alternates default `"•"` and DIM `"◦"` using `(elapsed_ms / 600) % 2 == 0`; absent start time means zero elapsed. Truecolor uses BOLD RGB `"•"`, synchronized to the shimmer helper's process-global start, not call start. Seconds come from `Duration::as_secs_f32()`; all floating-point constants and intermediates are **f32**, including `std::f32::consts::PI`: `position=floor(((seconds % 2.0)/2.0)*21.0)`, `distance=abs(10-position) as f32`, `t=0.5*(1.0+cos(PI*(distance/5.0)))` for distance ≤5, else zero. The nonnegative f32-to-integer position cast truncates; the distance subtraction occurs in integers before conversion to f32. Preserve the division-before-multiplication order inside the cosine. Set `alpha=clamp(t,0,1)*0.9`; each RGB component is `(background*alpha + foreground*(1.0-alpha)) as u8`, preserving f32 rounding before truncation. Missing foreground/background use `(128,128,128)`/`(255,255,255)`. At `t=1`, background `(0,0,0)` and foreground `(250,250,250)` yield `(25,25,25)`; evaluating with doubles and then truncating instead yields `(24,24,24)`. This counterexample is source-derived arithmetic, not a Codex execution. Background style stays unset. [Marker selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/motion.rs#L38-L79) · [shimmer constants](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/shimmer.rs#L14-L69) · [blend](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/color.rs#L7-L12)

### Command wrapping and URL exception

The exact four-column gutters are `"  │ "` = two spaces + U+2502 + space, `"  └ "` = two spaces + U+2514 + space, and `"    "` = four spaces. For total width `W`, command-continuation/output content width is `max(1, W-4)` using saturating subtraction. First command-line width is `max(1, W-header_prefix_width)`, where `header_prefix_width` uses Ratatui’s unadjusted `Line::width()`, distinct from Codex’s corrected wrapping widths. These fixed prefixes are unaffected by that distinction: ordinary prefixes occupy 10 (`Running`), 6 (`Ran`), or 10 (`You ran`) columns; interaction prefix occupies 2. [Layout constants](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L651-L700) · [header widths](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L365-L407)

Highlight first. Wrap the **entire first source line** at the reduced header width: first segment joins the header; its remaining segments retain that narrower width. Wrap subsequent source lines at `max(1,W-4)`. Keep the first two continuation entries; if more remain append `"… +N lines"`, where `N=continuation_count-2`, and prefix all retained continuation/ellipsis entries with `"  │ "`. The ellipsis is additional to the two-entry allowance, always says plural `lines`, and has no transcript hint. Neither it nor URL overflow receives a command-row budget. [Wrapping and continuation truncation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L387-L431) [and helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L499-L511)

`RtOptions` defaults to LF, FirstFit, `break_words=true`, `WordSeparator::new()`, HyphenSplitter; compact command/output explicitly select NoHyphenation. Exploration/transcript keep defaults. Adaptive wrapping flattens spans for detection, then slices original spans, preserving styles. No-URL lines follow textwrap; URL-only lines use AsciiSpace, NoHyphenation, `break_words=false`. Mixed prose/URL lines greedily pack words, preserve original interword spacing, keep URLs indivisible, and split oversized non-URL words by grapheme/display width. Leading spaces count on the first row; continuation leading spaces are skipped. An indivisible overwide grapheme still makes progress. Halfwidth sound marks U+FF9E/U+FF9F each add a cell beyond `UnicodeWidthStr`; standard wrapping projects affected graphemes to equal-width placeholders and maps ranges back. [Defaults](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L799-L810) · [adaptive dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L688-L761) · [standard/mixed wrapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L864-L1118) · [sound-mark projection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L43-L163) · [display width](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/width.rs#L17-L24)

URL detection splits ASCII whitespace and trims only `()[]{}<>,.;:!'"` from token ends - not backticks. Tokens containing `://` qualify when URL parsing succeeds (HTTP/HTTPS/FTP/FTPS/WS/WSS additionally require a host), or when parsing fails but the scheme is ASCII letter followed by ASCII alphanumeric/`+-.` and the remainder is nonempty. Bare forms require `/`, `?`, or `#`, unless host starts case-insensitive `www.`; host must be localhost, four dot-separated `u8`-parseable IPv4 components, or domain labels of 1–63 ASCII alphanumeric/hyphen characters with alphanumeric ends and a 2–63 ASCII-letter TLD. This is **not a registered-TLD list**. Numeric ports require 1–5 digits and successful `u16` parsing. Bare bracketed IPv6 is unsupported. Mixed-prose detection ignores empty/punctuation-only tokens, the raw markers `- * + • ◦ ▪ > | │ ┆ └ ├ ┌ ┐ ┘ ┼`, and ordered markers whose trimmed token is all ASCII digits and whose raw token ends in `.` or `)`. A remaining non-URL token must contain an alphanumeric character. [Exact predicates](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L437-L679)

An intact URL is one returned `Line`, potentially many viewport rows. It is not proof of a terminal hyperlink. Exec does not override the default plain hyperlink projection. [Default projection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L190-L197) · [URL assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L1032-L1063)

### Output selection: logical budget, then viewport budget

Ordinary/interaction calls use `L=5`; UserShell uses `L=50`. Output absent produces no body. Present-but-empty output produces `"  └ (no output)"` except for interactions. An empty logical line is output and is not this placeholder. [Limits and body dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L433-L490)

First, `output_lines` selects retained logical lines. Final aggregates use Rust `str::lines()` for both counting and iteration: LF/CRLF terminators disappear, a terminal newline adds no extra line, and an empty string has zero lines. Live counts instead retain total-versus-stored counts. [Output accessors](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L35-L60)

Let `T=total_lines`, `R=retained_lines`; all subtraction below saturates:

```text
head = min(T, L, R)
tail = min(T-head, L, R-head)
omitted = T-head-tail
result = first(head) + [ellipsis if omitted>0] + last(tail)
```

Maximum is `2L+1` entries, not `L`. Each selected line is independently ANSI-decoded; tabs become exactly four spaces, not tab stops; every resulting span gains DIM. No ANSI state is carried between logical lines by this caller. The helper also supports `only_err` (hide exit-zero output), `include_prefix`, and `include_angle_pipe`; compact exec passes all three `false`. With prefixes enabled: first head prefix is `"  └ "` only when angle-pipe is enabled, otherwise `"    "`; remaining head/tail prefixes are `"    "`, all DIM. The helper's own ellipsis remains unprefixed. At `L=0`, nonempty output produces only an omission marker. [Logical selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L103-L174) · [ANSI helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/ansi-escape/src/lib.rs#L6-L44)

The output omission literal is `"… +N lines (ctrl + t to view transcript)"`, including for `N=1`; this is a static hint string. Wrap **all** selected entries, including this marker, at `max(1,W-4)`, then prefix first entry `"  └ "`, later entries `"    "`. Apply `truncate_lines_middle` to these prefixed lines with budget `B=5` or `50` and original omitted count as a hint. [Hint literal](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/ui_consts.rs#L10-L12) · [ellipsis format](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L247-L253) · [wrap/prefix/truncate order](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L459-L490)

Exact second-stage algorithm (`n=input entry count`, `h=hint or 0`, `q=1 if hint present else 0`):

1. Clamp measurement width to at least 1. `B=0` returns nothing.
2. Cost each entry using `Paragraph.wrap(trim=false).line_count(W)`, minimum 1. Whitespace-only entries instead cost `max(1,ceil(line.width/W))`.
3. If total cost ≤B, return unchanged.
4. Estimate omissions as `h + saturating_sub(n,q)`. Measure replacement marker **with four-space DIM prefix** using Paragraph wrapping. If marker cost ≥B, return only that marker, even if it exceeds B.
5. Remaining budget is B minus estimated marker cost. Head gets floor(remaining/2), tail gets the rest. Greedily retain contiguous whole entries from each end; stop each side at its first non-fitting entry. Do not transfer unused head budget to tail or split an entry.
6. Return head, replacement marker, tail. Actual marker count is `h + saturating_sub(saturating_sub(n,head_count+tail_count),q)`. Do not rerun selection after the actual count changes.

[Row-cost algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L528-L618) · [marker measurement](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L625-L647)

The final count is **not guaranteed to mean original newline-delimited lines**: it combines the upstream logical omission count with removed post-wrap entries, subtracting exactly one for an existing hint even when that hint wrapped into several entries. A multirow preserved URL counts as one entry. No marker-text inspection removes old markers from retained head/tail. Narrow-width fallback may exceed the target row budget and may contain no branch glyph. [Counting and fallback code](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L564-L618)

### Live retention and authoritative final replacement

| Constant                              | Exact value                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- |
| `LIVE_COMMAND_OUTPUT_MAX_BYTES`       | 1,048,576 UTF-8 bytes           |
| `LIVE_COMMAND_OUTPUT_MAX_LINES`       | 50                              |
| `LIVE_COMMAND_OUTPUT_MAX_LINE_BYTES`  | floor(1,048,576 / 102) = 10,280 |
| `LIVE_COMMAND_OUTPUT_LINE_HEAD_BYTES` | 5,140                           |
| `LIVE_COMMAND_OUTPUT_LINE_TAIL_BYTES` | 5,140                           |

[Constants](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output.rs#L5-L11)

Until accumulated bytes would exceed 1 MiB, store the complete string - including exactly 1 MiB. Count LF bytes as completed lines; a nonempty chunk sets partial-line presence from whether it ends in LF. On crossing, permanently switch to bounded mode, move out and reprocess prior full output, then process the incoming chunk. Keep first 50 completed lines, a deque of last 50 later completed lines, and an optional in-progress line: at most 101 retained logical lines. `total=completed+partial`; bounded `retained=head.len+tail.len+partial`. This is retained-payload control, not a process-memory bound including string capacities, incoming chunks, transition temporaries, or finalized output. [Storage transition and counting](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output.rs#L19-L113)

Bounded ingestion splits inclusively on LF. Remove one CR immediately before LF; hold a chunk-final CR pending so a following LF terminates CRLF, while a subsequent non-LF preserves that CR. Rendering a partial line appends its pending CR. Lone/repeated CRs are retained, not implemented as terminal cursor-overwrite operations. [CRLF algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output.rs#L55-L96) · [partial rendering](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output.rs#L161-L168)

Per-line retention fills head only while tail is empty and omitted bytes are zero. Head cut retreats to a UTF-8 boundary; tail cuts advance to a UTF-8 boundary. After filling head, a remaining chunk of at least 5,140 bytes replaces tail with its bounded suffix and counts old tail plus dropped new prefix as omitted; smaller remainders append then drain excess from the front. Omission counts saturate. Render `head + optional ANSI repair/reset + "... N bytes omitted ..." + tail`. For an omitted line, inspect the last ESC in head: OSC beginning `]` with no BEL gets BEL appended; CSI beginning `[` with no ASCII alphabetic byte afterward gets `m` appended. Then always insert `ESC[0m` before the marker. No separator/newline beyond the literal marker and no restoration of the omitted style state precedes tail. [Per-line algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output.rs#L172-L254)

Before storage truncation, compact `lines()` already abbreviates individual lines exceeding 10,280 bytes, but `transcript_lines()` returns original full lines. After truncation, transcript is retained head, optional `"… +N lines"` storage marker, retained tail, partial line; `N=total-retained`. That marker has no transcript shortcut and reaches ANSI parsing as ordinary text, with no dedicated DIM style in transcript. Byte omissions do not increase logical-line counts. Final `CommandOutput::new(exit, aggregate)` has no live buffer; completion replaces the preview entirely, so final content need not equal concatenated deltas and can exceed these local live limits. [Projection differences](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output.rs#L115-L158) · [final object and replacement](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L26-L60) [and](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L120-L133)

### Transcript and raw projections

Transcript iterates **every original call**, with one empty line between calls. Commands use Magenta `"$ "` initially, default `"    "` thereafter, adaptive wrapping at supplied width, and no continuation cap. Output uses its own unprefixed full-width adaptive wrapping at `max(1,W)`, without compact budgets or forced DIM; interactions omit it. A footer exists only when both output and duration exist: `"✓ • {duration}"` for zero, `"✗ ({exit}) • {duration}"` otherwise. There is no pending footer or `(no output)` placeholder. Raw is `plain_lines(transcript_lines(65535))`: concatenate each line's span text, then pass that string to `Line::from` to construct a fresh default-style **line**. The locked Ratatui constructor maps `str::lines()` fragments to adjacent spans without separator text; any LF/CRLF separators in the concatenated text disappear, and the fresh line can have zero, one, or multiple spans. An empty string produces zero spans. Raw retains `$`, status glyphs, durations, and storage omissions; it is neither original argv nor untouched output bytes, and extremely long lines can still wrap at 65,535. [All projections](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L195-L243) · [style stripping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L163-L175) · [owned-string splitting](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L222-L226) · [raw constructor](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L251-L258) · [String conversion](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L605-L608)

Duration uses integer milliseconds: below 1,000, `"{ms}ms"`; below 60,000, seconds with two decimals; otherwise integer minutes plus zero-padded remaining seconds, `"{minutes}m {seconds:02}s"`. Thus 59,999ms → `"60.00s"`, 60,000ms → `"1m 00s"`, and one hour → `"60m 00s"`. Desired compact/raw/transcript heights follow the shared default contract: Paragraph wrapping with `trim=false` measures viewport rows, including overwide URLs, then **`.try_into().unwrap_or(0)`** converts the count to `u16`. Heights of **65,536 or greater report 0**; the line vector is unchanged. Neither unbounded exploration accumulation nor the absence of compact budgets in expanded output removes this height-conversion behavior. [Duration implementation/assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/utils/elapsed/src/lib.rs#L9-L71) · [height measurement and conversion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L223-L264)

### Source-backed worked examples

**Mixed-call reads remain separate.** A completed call containing Search(`shimmer_spans`), Read(`shimmer.rs`), Read(`status_indicator_widget.rs`) produces the following at width 80; despite its test name, the committed assertion does not coalesce those reads. Header title is BOLD, bullet DIM, labels Cyan, payloads default. [Fixture construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L1901-L1940) · [committed text](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/snapshots/codex_tui__history_cell__tests__coalesces_sequential_reads_within_one_call.snap#L5-L8)

```text
• Explored
  └ Search shimmer_spans
    Read shimmer.rs
    Read status_indicator_widget.rs
```

**First-line width persists into its continuations.** At width 24, completed command `a_very_long_token_without_spaces_to_force_wrapping` uses 18-column chunks (`24-6`), even though later gutter-prefixed rows could hold 20. The success bullet is Green+BOLD; gutters/placeholder DIM; command styles come from Bash highlighting. [Construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L2083-L2104) · [committed text](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/snapshots/codex_tui__history_cell__tests__single_line_command_wraps_with_four_space_continuation.snap#L5-L8)

```text
• Ran a_very_long_token_
  │ without_spaces_to_
  │ force_wrapping
  └ (no output)
```

**Ten short output lines, exit 1, width 80.** Logical selection keeps all ten; visual budget reserves one marker row, leaving two head/two tail rows. Only the bullet is failure-red; ordinary numeric output remains default foreground + DIM. [Construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L2177-L2216) · [committed text](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/snapshots/codex_tui__history_cell__tests__stderr_tail_more_than_five_lines_snapshot.snap#L5-L10)

```text
• Ran seq 1 10 1>&2 && false
  └ 1
    2
    … +6 lines (ctrl + t to view transcript)
    9
    10
```

Additional committed assertions verify `L=2` over seven logical lines gives `1, 2, marker(+3), 6, 7`; 100,000 newline bytes give eleven helper entries and 99,990 omissions; and bounded storage over 180 completed long lines plus a partial line retains 101 lines with `"… +80 lines"` at transcript position 50. UTF-8 head/tail, split CRLF, and truncated OSC tail visibility have dedicated assertions. [Logical-budget assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L846-L894) · [live-buffer assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output_tests.rs#L32-L135)

## MCP calls

### Contract and lifecycle

This chapter describes observed Codex behavior at `121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df`, not a proposed Pi API. Quoted literals use JSON-style escapes: `" "` means one U+0020 space, `"\n"` means LF; `•` is U+2022, `◦` U+25E6, `└` U+2514, and `…` U+2026. ASCII `"..."` is a different truncation marker.

`McpToolCallCell` retains `call_id`, `{server, tool, arguments}`, a local `Instant`, optional duration, optional `Result<McpToolResult, String>`, and `animations_enabled`. Construction sets both optional fields to `None`. Completion projects the wire result once, stores duration/result, and optionally returns a separate image-notice cell. Duration is retained but never printed in any of this cell’s three projections. [State and completion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L42-L125), [renderers](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L135-L321).

Both start and completion enter the interrupt queue whenever an answer stream exists **or the queue is already nonempty**; stream completion flushes it FIFO. Start flushes the answer stream and previous active cell, creates the MCP cell with `arguments: Some(arguments)` and the local animation setting, increments the active revision, and requests redraw. Completion updates only an active MCP cell with the same ID; otherwise it flushes the current active cell and constructs a completed cell from the completion payload. A matching cell keeps its original invocation. The call is then committed, followed by any image notice, and `had_work_activity` becomes true. There is no map of independently mutable MCP cards or update of an already committed card in this handler. Flushing alone neither completes nor freezes a pending cell: overlapping starts can commit a still-`Calling` row, with its later completion rendered as a new cell. [Flush operation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1199-L1205).
