[Queue rule](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/streaming.rs#L444-L476), [MCP routing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L55-L69), [start/completion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L167-L258).

The completion adapter computes `Duration::from_millis(max(duration_ms.unwrap_or(0), 0))` and applies this precedence before rendering:

| Completion payload                             | Stored result                                       |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `error` present, with or without `result`      | `Err(error.message)`; supplied content is discarded |
| `result` present, no `error`, status `Failed`  | `Ok(projected content)`, `is_error = true`          |
| `result` present, no `error`, any other status | `Ok(projected content)`, `is_error = false`         |
| Neither                                        | `Err("MCP tool call completed without a result")`   |

Thus a failed call can retain useful content without acquiring an `"Error: "` prefix. Direct construction from protocol `CallToolResult` instead uses `is_error.unwrap_or(false)`. Neither body wording nor a JSON `exit_code` determines the call’s success. [Adapter](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L196-L229), [projected error flag](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L103-L108).

| Cell/event state               | Verb; bullet                 | Details and animation                                                         |
| ------------------------------ | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `result = None`                | `Calling`; active indicator  | No result body; tick only if animations enabled                               |
| `Some(Ok)`, `is_error = false` | `Called`; bold green `•`     | Project all blocks; eligible REPL success envelopes may simplify              |
| `Some(Ok)`, `is_error = true`  | `Called`; bold red `•`       | Project all blocks; no REPL success simplification                            |
| `Some(Err(message))`           | `Called`; bold red `•`       | `"Error: " + message`; no image notice                                        |
| `mark_failed()`                | `Called`; bold red `•`       | Overwrites result with `Err("interrupted")`, duration with local elapsed time |
| `freeze_snapshot()`            | Existing state/verb retained | Sets animations false only; a pending row stays `Calling`                     |
| Replay `InProgress` item       | Start path                   | Remains active; does not manufacture a missing-result error                   |
| Replay other MCP status        | Completion path              | Same normalization/commit behavior as live completion                         |

[Cell truth table](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L109-L154), [replay dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L202-L206). Turn finalization invokes `mark_failed()` on the active MCP cell and commits it; interruption calls that finalizer. Despite comments mentioning `✗`, MCP’s actual failed glyph is `•`. External-writer viewing freezes the active MCP snapshot. [Finalization](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1390-L1402), [turn cleanup](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/turn_runtime.rs#L315-L335), [interruption](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/input_restore.rs#L235-L244), [freeze caller](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1751-L1764).

`McpToolCallProgress` is ignored by the widget notification dispatcher: no streamed MCP body, progress text, percentage, or progress bar is rendered. [Ignored notifications](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol.rs#L238-L282).

### Literal spans, styles, and motion

Here **default** means no explicit foreground/background override (`None`), not hardcoded white/black. All call lines have default line style; every span below has **unset background** and no modifiers except those listed. The call renderer adds no box, border, background fill, Markdown parsing, syntax highlighting, or explicit hyperlink annotation. [Call composition](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L135-L277), [default hyperlink projection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L185-L218).

| Span                                                                     | Foreground              | Modifiers |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- | --------- |
| Successful `"•"`                                                         | `Color::Green` directly | `BOLD`    |
| Failed `"•"`                                                             | `Color::Red` directly   | `BOLD`    |
| `"Calling"` / `"Called"`                                                 | default                 | `BOLD`    |
| Header separators `" "`                                                  | default                 | none      |
| Full invocation server and tool, separately                              | `Color::Cyan` directly  | none      |
| Invocation `"."`, `"("`, `")"`                                           | default                 | none      |
| Invocation serialized arguments                                          | default                 | `DIM`     |
| Compact REPL title, including fallback `server.tool`                     | `Color::Cyan`           | none      |
| Tree prefix `"  └ "`                                                     | default                 | `DIM`     |
| Outer continuation `"    "` and wrapper continuation `"    "`            | default                 | none      |
| Every result/error text segment, including summaries and truncation dots | default                 | `DIM`     |
| Separate `"tool result (image output)"` notice                           | default                 | none      |

[Header/styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L135-L190), [details/styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L194-L274), [invocation spans](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L727-L746), [notice](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L21-L32).

Pending calls request `activity_indicator(Some(start_time), mode, StaticBullet)`. Reduced motion gives dim default-foreground `•`. Animated non-truecolor output uses default unmodified `•` when `floor(elapsed_ms / 600)` is even, dim default `◦` when odd. Animated truecolor output takes the first span of shared `shimmer_spans("•")`: an RGB-foreground, bold span with unset background. Its shared RGB sweep algorithm is outside this chapter. The caller’s defensive `None` fallback is dim `•`. Completion bypasses motion entirely; the direct green/red completion colors do not use inventory’s semantic palette fallbacks. [Motion dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/motion.rs#L22-L49), [animated indicator](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/motion.rs#L65-L80), [RGB span modifiers](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/shimmer.rs#L52-L66).

The transcript cache tick is `Some((elapsed.as_millis() / 50) as u64)` exactly when animations are enabled and the result is absent; otherwise `None`. It invalidates the cached active transcript tail, not a displayed elapsed-time field. [Tick](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L315-L321), [cache key consumer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1905-L1929).

### Invocation and width algorithm

Full invocation is `server + "." + tool + "(" + args + ")"`. `args` is `serde_json::to_string(value)` with `value.to_string()` fallback; absent arguments produce `""`. Consequently `None` gives `s.t()`, `Some(null)` gives `s.t(null)`, and `{}` gives `s.t({})`. Arguments are neither truncated nor prettified with separator spaces. TUI’s `serde_json` enables `preserve_order`; do not independently alphabetize object keys. [Formatter](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L727-L746), [dependency feature](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/Cargo.toml#L96-L105).

For supplied width `W`:

1. Construct `[bullet, " ", bold verb, " "]`. Its unadjusted Ratatui `Line::width()` is the reserved width: normally 10 columns for `Calling`, 9 for `Called`.
2. If the invocation’s unadjusted `Line::width()` is `<= W.saturating_sub(reserved)`, append it to that header. Both measurements return `usize` sums of span Unicode widths, without Codex’s halfwidth-sound-mark correction.
3. Otherwise remove the header’s final space. Wrap invocation with `RtOptions(width = W.saturating_sub(4), initial_indent = "", subsequent_indent = "    ")`; then prefix the first row with dim `"  └ "`, later rows with default `"    "`.
4. Set detail width `D = max(W.saturating_sub(4), 1)`. Select/format each block as specified below. Split each selected result string on LF, including empty segments. Wrap **each segment independently** with width `D`, empty initial indent, four-space subsequent indent; segment text is dim.
5. Prefix the combined details only once: first detail row gets dim `"  └ "` if invocation was inline, otherwise default `"    "`; every later detail row gets default `"    "`.

The tree prefix is exactly two ASCII spaces, `└`, then one ASCII space; each indentation literal is four ASCII spaces. Using zero-based columns, a new source line or block normally starts at column 4; its soft-wrapped continuations start at column 8. The tree branch belongs to the invocation **or** the first detail, never to every content block. No blank separator is inserted between blocks. An empty content vector adds no body or `(no output)` placeholder; an empty text block normally still contributes an empty detail row. [Exact layout](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L171-L274), [prefix helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/line_utils.rs#L55-L76).

The fit test and subsequent wrapping use different width APIs. Codex’s `line_width()` sums corrected `display_width()` per span, adding a cell per U+FF9E/U+FF9F; the wrapper uses these corrected helpers. Ratatui’s `Line::width()` does not add those cells, and is distinct from Ratatui’s `u16`-returning `CellWidth::cell_width()`. Source-derived fit counterexample: a completed standard invocation with `server="s"`, `tool="t"`, arguments equal to the JSON string `"ｶﾞ"`, and `W=17` displays invocation text `s.t("ｶﾞ")`. `Line::width()` measures that invocation as 8, so it stays inline beside the 9-column completed header. Corrected display width measures the invocation as 9; a port using corrected width for the fit test would put it below the header. This is a source-derived fit decision, not an interactively observed screenshot. [Fit and wrapping calls](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L171-L190), [formatter and span boundaries](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L727-L746), [corrected span sum](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/line_truncation.rs#L7-L11), [corrected display width](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/width.rs#L17-L33), [wrapper measurement](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L881-L915), [Ratatui line width](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L441-L443), [unadjusted span width](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/span.rs#L379-L386), [bounded cell width](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/buffer/cell_width.rs#L19-L44).

Transport/error strings are an exception to step 4: construct `"Error: " + err`, truncate with width **W**, not D, then call `Line::from(err_text.dim())` and pass that one dim span to the wrapper. This uses the **Span conversion**, retaining any line separators in the span’s text before wrapping, unlike raw errors’ String conversion below. REPL transcript errors bypass truncation. This path does not explicitly split LF into independent wrapper calls; ordinary result bodies do. [Error branch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L244-L261), [result-body splitting](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L231-L242), [Ratatui conversion implementations](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L605-L629).

`RtOptions` uses LF, `FirstFit`, `break_words = true`, `WordSeparator::new()`, and `HyphenSplitter`. The wrapper preserves source span styles, reserves indent width with a minimum of one remaining cell, and can therefore exceed extremely narrow W. Adaptive wrapping recognizes URL-like tokens across span boundaries: no URL uses ordinary wrapping; URL-only lines use ASCII-space separation, no hyphenation, no word breaking; mixed lines preserve URL tokens but split overlong non-URL tokens by grapheme/display width. Detection splits ASCII whitespace and trims surrounding `()[]{}<>,.;:!'"` characters. Schemed tokens require `://`: parsed HTTP/HTTPS/FTP/FTPS/WS/WSS require a host; other parsed schemes pass, with an ASCII scheme-prefix fallback for parse failures. Bare hosts require `/`, `?`, or `#`, unless prefixed `www.`; accept localhost, four u8 IPv4 components, or domain labels of 1–63 ASCII alphanumeric/hyphen characters with alphanumeric endpoints and a 2–63-letter TLD. Numeric ports must fit u16 and use at most five digits. Thus ordinary `src/main.rs` is not a URL; bare IPv6 is not recognized. Preserved overlong URLs can exceed logical width and are measured by the outer paragraph’s viewport wrapping before the shared `u16` desired-height conversion, which reports 0 on overflow. [Options](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L798-L810), [URL predicate](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L474-L681), [adaptive dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L683-L728), [ordinary span wrapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L873-L946), [mixed wrapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L959-L1135), [outer height](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L221-L244).

Ordinary wrapping also projects graphemes containing halfwidth sound marks U+FF9E/U+FF9F into width-preserving placeholders, then maps wrapped byte ranges back to source without separating one projected source grapheme. Reusing only generic word wrapping would omit this behavior. [Projection/range mapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L43-L233), [trimmed-range dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L280-L289).

### Content projection and image validity

At completion, each `content` JSON value is deserialized by reference as `rmcp::model::ContentBlock`. Retained content is width-independent; resizing reruns formatting on retained full text/fallback JSON, but media/resource bodies are discarded. `structured_content` and `_meta` are not retained or rendered by this projection - even a structured-only successful result produces just its header. [Projection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L25-L110).

| Deserialized variant                                       | Retained display                    | Width formatting                                    |
| ---------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| Text                                                       | Exact `text.text`                   | JSON compaction plus truncation                     |
| Image                                                      | `"<image content>"`                 | Summary untruncated; separate decode check          |
| Audio                                                      | `"<audio content>"`                 | Summary untruncated; no audio decode/playback       |
| Embedded text or blob resource                             | `"embedded resource: " + uri`       | Summary untruncated; body/MIME omitted              |
| Other embedded resource variant                            | `"<unknown embedded resource>"`     | Summary untruncated                                 |
| Resource link                                              | `"link: " + uri`                    | Summary untruncated; name/title/description omitted |
| Unknown/unhandled variant or **any deserialization error** | Original JSON value’s `to_string()` | JSON compaction plus truncation                     |

The fallback is serialized JSON, not original wire whitespace. Invalid annotations can make an otherwise text-looking block fall back to JSON; missing required image metadata can expose serialized image data through that same fallback. The committed projection test covers an image missing `mimeType`, text with `annotations.priority = "high"`, and `type = "future_block"`, at widths 1, 8, 40, 120, and 10,000. [Variant mapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L54-L100), [render branches](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L127-L137), [committed vectors](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_tests.rs#L131-L179).

For each successfully parsed image until one decodes successfully:

1. If data starts with literal `"data:"`, remove that prefix and use everything after the **first comma**; no comma returns `None`. The URL’s MIME/base64 declaration is not checked.
2. Decode with standard Base64, create `ImageReader(Cursor(bytes))`, call `with_guessed_format()`, then fully `decode()` pixel data.
3. OR that success into `has_image`; after the first success stop decoding later images, but continue projecting all blocks.

Failures log `"Failed to decode image data: {e}"`, `"Failed to guess image format: {e}"`, or `"Image decoding failed: {e}"`; these are tracing errors, not visible body notices. Parsed-but-corrupt images still display `<image content>` and do not change call success. Any `Ok` result with `has_image`, including `is_error = true`, produces exactly one extra unstyled `tool result (image output)` cell after the call. No pixels, image dimensions, media viewer, or link are rendered by that cell. [Decode algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L140-L171), [short circuit](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L70-L76), [notice creation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L93-L107), [notice text](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L21-L32). A committed vector truncates a valid PNG to its first 33 bytes: no marker; adding a valid data-URL PNG afterward makes `has_image` true. [Image validity test](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_tests.rs#L181-L196).

### JSON formatting and per-block truncation

`TOOL_CALL_MAX_LINES = 5` is a **grapheme-budget multiplier**, not a five-row card limit. For formatter width B, compute `G = (5 * B).saturating_sub(5)`. Ordinary result blocks use B = D; ordinary errors use B = W. Each block gets its own full budget; media/resource summaries bypass it. [Constant](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L33-L34), [budget](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/text_formatting.rs#L17-L34).

Formatting first attempts to parse the **entire** string as `serde_json::Value`, including primitives. On success it pretty-serializes, then scans characters with `in_string` and `escape_next`: unescaped quotes toggle string state; backslashes inside strings toggle escape state; LF/CR outside strings disappear; outside-string spaces/tabs survive only immediately after `:` or `,` and when the next character is not `}` or `]`; other characters are copied, resetting an outstanding escape inside strings. Failed JSON parsing leaves source text unchanged. Thus `{"a":"b","c":["d","e"]}` becomes `{"a": "b", "c": ["d", "e"]}`, while the invocation formatter remains separator-space-free. [Exact compactor](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/text_formatting.rs#L36-L86).

Truncation counts extended graphemes. At `length <= G`, return unchanged. Otherwise keep the first `G - 3` graphemes and append ASCII `"..."` when `G >= 3`; for G = 0, 1, or 2, return that many graphemes without dots. Newlines count toward the budget but are not a row cap. Wide graphemes and continuation indentation further invalidate any five-screen-row assumption. [Truncator](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/text_formatting.rs#L88-L114). Committed assertions include `Hello → "" / "H" / "He" / "..."` at limits 0/1/2/3, and five emoji at limit 4 → `"👋..."`. [Boundary vectors](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/text_formatting.rs#L370-L430).

### Node REPL predicates and envelopes

The specialization is exactly `(server == "node_repl" || server == "cua_repl") && tool == "js"`, case-sensitive, using raw server names. No namespace stripping occurs here; the protocol’s broader `is_node_repl_backed_tool` predicate is **not** the predicate this cell calls. [Cell predicate](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L127-L139), [raw-server predicate and distinct namespace helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/protocol/src/mcp.rs#L38-L54).

Normal display uses a cyan title in pending, successful, and failed states:

1. Read string-valued `arguments.title`.
2. Apply Rust `split_whitespace()`, join tokens with one ASCII space.
3. Reject empty normalized text; otherwise retain the first **80 extended graphemes**, without ellipsis or a post-truncation trim.
4. Missing/non-string/empty titles fall back to `server.tool`, **without parentheses**.

Transcript and raw views always use the full invocation. [Title algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L156-L170).

REPL projection additionally retains a string-valued top-level `text` on malformed or non-text blocks. Valid text blocks reuse their normal text storage. `block.text()` returns either that valid text or the retained top-level field; standard MCP never retains the extra field. [Retention and accessor](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L59-L69), [accessor](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_result.rs#L112-L125).

Only **successful compact display** tries to extract meaningful output, independently per block:

| Text condition                                                                    | Selected output                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| Starts exactly `"Script completed\n"` and contains `"\nOutput:\n"`                | Suffix after first delimiter                               |
| Starts `"Script completed\n"`, delimiter missing                                  | Extraction fails; do **not** try JSON afterward            |
| Otherwise deserializes as `{exit_code: i64, output: String}`, `exit_code == 0`    | `output`; additional fields such as `chunk_id` are ignored |
| No top-level text, malformed envelope, nonzero exit, or wrong/missing field types | Extraction fails                                           |

Extracted `""` skips the block entirely; whitespace-only output is not skipped. Nonempty extraction goes through JSON compaction/truncation at D. Extraction failure falls back to normal `block.render(D)`, **not** to the extra top-level text. Failed compact calls skip extraction entirely. REPL transcript instead uses `block.text().trim_end_matches('\n')` without JSON formatting/truncation whenever text is available, even for failures/non-text blocks; it removes all trailing LF, not CR. Blocks without text still use ordinary rendering. [Envelope type](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L67-L71), [selection algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L196-L242).

### Compact, Ctrl+T, raw, and persisted projections

| Projection                               | Invocation                                                             | Result body                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Standard compact / Ctrl+T                | Full, styled, adaptive wrapping                                        | Same per-block formatting/truncation in both                                               |
| REPL compact                             | Normalized title/fallback                                              | Success-envelope extraction only on success                                                |
| REPL Ctrl+T                              | Full, styled                                                           | Available top-level text untruncated, trailing LF removed; non-text fallback still bounded |
| Call-cell raw scrollback, including REPL | Unstyled `"Calling "` or `"Called "` + full invocation; no bullet/tree | Always `block.render(10_000)`; no title/envelope specialization                            |

All three projections inherit the shared desired-height contract: `Paragraph.wrap(trim=false).line_count(width)` followed by **`.try_into().unwrap_or(0)`** to `u16`. A measured height of **65,536 or greater reports 0**, without changing the line vector. Independent per-block budgets do not impose a total card limit or prevent this conversion overflow. [Rich/raw and transcript height conversions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L223-L264).

Raw’s text/JSON budget is **49,995 graphemes per block**, not unlimited. `raw_lines_from_source` splits LF, removes exactly one final empty part when source ends in LF, preserves internal/trailing blank lines beyond that, and returns zero lines for empty source. Raw errors instead append one unstyled `Line::from("Error: " + err)` without truncation or caller-side splitting. The locked Ratatui **String conversion** internally uses `str::lines()` to create adjacent spans on that one line, inserting no separators: error `"alpha\nbeta"` gives visible raw text **`"Error: alphabeta"`**. Error `"alpha\r\nbeta"` gives the same text because both CRLF bytes disappear. These are constructor-derived examples, not terminal observations. A fresh `Line::from("")` has zero spans; String conversion need not produce exactly one span. All raw styles are default; there is no call-cell ANSI decoder or general sanitizer beyond such constructor effects. [Raw renderer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L280-L313), [raw width](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L102-L103), [LF helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L147-L161), [owned-string splitting](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L222-L226), [raw constructor](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L251-L258), [String conversion](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L605-L608). The raw **result-body** test explicitly asserts unstyled lines and `alpha\nbeta → ["alpha", "beta"]`; it does not describe the raw error constructor. [Style assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L426-L454).

Two persisted-history paths must not be confused with this cell’s Ctrl+T:

- `thread_transcript`’s fallback cell contains only dim `"mcp tool: {server}/{tool} · {status:?}"`, with statuses `InProgress`, `Completed`, or `Failed`; no invocation arguments or body. [Fallback](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/thread_transcript.rs#L246-L255), [status enum](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/app-server-protocol/src/protocol/v2/item.rs#L1186-L1193).
- Persisted conversation export reconstructs an unstyled activity cell headed `"mcp tool: {server}/{tool}({arguments}) · {status:?}"`. Text is LF-split without compaction/truncation; image/audio use summaries; resources use `embedded resource: URI` (missing URI fallback `<unknown embedded resource>`); links use `link: URI`; invalid blocks use serialized JSON. It also appends `"structured result: {content}"` and, independently, `"error: {message}"` in lowercase, so result and error can both appear. Persisted errors explicitly use `raw_lines_from_source`, preserving separate LF-delimited lines rather than concatenating them as the raw call-cell error constructor does. No image decode or extra marker occurs. Markdown export wraps it under `## Activity`, prefixes every line with four spaces, and sanitizes CSI/control characters while retaining tabs/LF. Ephemeral/history-unavailable fallback can instead export retained visible cells’ raw projections. [Loader/fallbacks](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/transcript_export.rs#L76-L130), [MCP export](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/transcript_export.rs#L146-L195), [Markdown envelope](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/transcript_export.rs#L222-L280), [sanitizer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/messages.rs#L20-L82).

### Worked outputs and committed regression vectors

At width 80, the committed standard success and transport-error fixtures render the following. The body remains dim in both; only the bold bullet changes green/red. Neither the 1,420 ms success duration nor 2-second error duration appears. [Test inputs](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L1469-L1500), [error input](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L1596-L1620), [success snapshot](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/snapshots/codex_tui__history_cell__tests__completed_mcp_tool_call_success_snapshot.snap#L5-L6), [error snapshot](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/snapshots/codex_tui__history_cell__tests__completed_mcp_tool_call_error_snapshot.snap#L5-L6).

[Inputs](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L1622-L1663), [snapshot](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/snapshots/codex_tui__history_cell__tests__completed_mcp_tool_call_multiple_outputs_snapshot.snap#L5-L10).

The REPL width-40 fixture extracts a 216-character output (`"0123456789"` repeated 20 times plus `" transcript tail"`) from a successful exec envelope and drops an empty `Script completed` block. D = 36 gives G = 175: keep 172 graphemes plus three dots. It nevertheless occupies **six** result rows because continuations reserve four additional spaces. [Committed input/output](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L1352-L1402).

        45678901...

Additional committed vectors protect distinctions that plaintext snapshots alone cannot establish:

| Vector                                                                   | Asserted behavior                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Both REPL servers; `Failed` status with an `exit_code: 0` block          | Header is exactly red-bold bullet + bold `Called` + cyan title; JSON envelope stays visible with separator spaces, rather than collapsing to `ready`. [Assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/history_replay.rs#L1238-L1298)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Image and unknown block each carrying top-level `text`                   | Compact shows extracted `image-side output` / `unknown-side output`; transcript shows original envelopes; raw shows `<image content>` and fallback JSON. Same compact/transcript rule tested for `cua_repl`. [Vector](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_tests.rs#L198-L292)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| In-progress replay; deferred begin/end; external-writer snapshot         | Replay stays active without missing-result error; queued completion cannot overtake start; frozen MCP returns no animation tick. [Replay/FIFO](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/history_replay.rs#L1207-L1235), [FIFO completion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/history_replay.rs#L1301-L1365), [freeze](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L4-L47) |
| Persisted export with media, structured data, ANSI text, multiline error | Includes `structured result: {"count":1}`; excludes Base64 media bodies and ESC; error’s `# injected heading` is indented. [Assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/transcript_export_tests.rs#L119-L172)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### MCP inventory cells in the same module

Inventory is a separate renderer, not a call-result specialization. Its production source is app-server statuses sorted by server name, with no client-config enrichment. The similarly named `new_mcp_tools_output(config, …)` is guarded by `#[cfg(test)]`; its command/environment masking fixtures do not describe the production status-list display. [Test-only boundary](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L351-L360), [production contract](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L523-L550).

Loading is one width-independent line: active indicator, default `" "`, bold `"Loading MCP inventory"`, dim `"…"`. Raw is unstyled `"Loading MCP inventory..."`. The indicator uses the call’s motion rules; its tick is elapsed milliseconds / 50 whenever animations are enabled, with no result field. Display and transcript otherwise match. [Loading cell](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L678-L726).

Inventory success begins with magenta `/mcp`, blank line, `[default "🔌  ", bold "MCP Tools"]`, blank line. Each server row has spans `[state-styled "  • ", bold name, default ": ", state-styled label, dim " (N tool[s])"]`; singular only when N = 1. Background remains unset throughout. [Rows](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L538-L594).

| Runtime state                     | Literal label                            | State style              |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------ |
| Connected                         | `connected`                              | semantic success         |
| Starting                          | `starting`                               | shared accent            |
| AuthenticationRequired            | `authentication required`                | semantic attention       |
| Failed                            | `failed`                                 | semantic failure         |
| NotStarted / Disabled / Cancelled | `not started` / `disabled` / `cancelled` | default foreground + DIM |
| Absent, auth NotLoggedIn          | `authentication required`                | semantic attention       |
| Absent, other auth                | `unknown`                                | default foreground + DIM |

Semantic success/failure use green/red + BOLD, except unknown color capability uses explicit `Color::Reset`. Attention is yellow + BOLD only for a known dark background and known color capability; otherwise Reset + BOLD. Accent is cyan + BOLD except on light backgrounds, where it uses `best_color((0,95,135))` + BOLD. These are shared palette helpers, not call-bullet styles. [State mapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L553-L579), [status helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/style.rs#L13-L42), [accent helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/style.rs#L96-L102). Committed tests assert both bullet-prefix and label span styles for every state and verify that explicit Disabled overrides NotLoggedIn. [Style tests](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_tests.rs#L10-L119).

`ToolsAndAuthOnly` actually stops after each summary row; append a blank line and dim `"  Use /mcp verbose for tools and resources."`. `Full` adds the following unstyled fields, followed by a blank line per server:

- `"    • Auth: "` plus exactly `Unknown`, `Unsupported`, `Not logged in`, `Bearer token`, or `OAuth`.
- `"    • Tools: "` plus sorted tool-map keys joined by `", "`, or `(none)`.
- `"    • Resources: "` and `"    • Resource templates: "`: `(none)`, or source-order entries joined by `", "`. Each entry is default `title.unwrap_or(name)`, default `" "`, dim `"(URI)"` / `"(uri_template)"`; an empty title does not fall back.

Full mode with zero tools across all servers inserts italic `"  • No MCP tools available."` and a blank line before rows. These `PlainHistoryCell`s do not prewrap or truncate; transcript clones display, and raw strips styles but preserves visible text/prefixes. [Detail fields](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L548-L670), [auth literals](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L33-L41), [plain cell](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/base.rs#L5-L24).

Zero servers instead produces the same four-line heading, italic `"  • No MCP servers configured."`, then dim `"    See the MCP docs to configure them."`; only `MCP docs` additionally has UNDERLINED and an explicit hyperlink to `https://developers.openai.com/codex/mcp`. Raw retains that visible text without styles/hyperlinks. [Empty inventory](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L331-L349), [hyperlink-cell projections](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/base.rs#L43-L59).

Request start flushes prior active content and installs loading. A result for a different non-null origin thread is ignored. Otherwise cleanup removes loading only if it is still the active cell and removes the last committed loading copy from transcript history; unrelated active cells survive. Fetch failure calls `add_error_message("Failed to load MCP inventory: {err}")`: the generic error cell is one red, non-bold span `"■ Failed to load MCP inventory: {err}"`, with one ASCII space after U+25A0 and unset background; raw removes its style but keeps the square. This is not a failed MCP call. [Error helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/notices.rs#L244-L250), [forwarding method](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1531-L1533). [Request/active cleanup](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1541-L1577), [result/committed cleanup](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/background_requests.rs#L713-L765).

## Patches and diffs

### Contract and notation

`PatchHistoryCell` delegates to `diff_render`; hunk traversal lives inside `render_change`. The relevant implementations are [`patches.rs`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/patches.rs#L7-L57) and [`diff_render.rs`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L484-L746).

Quoted strings below use JSON notation; their quotes are not displayed. `SP(n)` means exactly `n` U+0020 spaces. `unset` means the corresponding Ratatui style field is `None`, not explicit white, black, or `Color::Reset`. Unless specified, foreground/background are unset, added/removed modifier sets are empty, and alignment is unset. Named colors remain terminal-palette colors; their actual RGB values are not fixed.

### Patch lifecycle and failure cells

`PatchHistoryCell` stores only `changes` and `cwd`: no call ID, pending flag, success flag, timer, spinner, or abort state. Start inserts it directly into history. Completion does not replace or recolor it. [Cell](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/patches.rs#L7-L36), [start](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L9-L12).

| Input/event                         | Patch-specific history effect                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Approval request                    | No proposed-patch history cell in the tested approval flow; approval is a separate modal.                                                                                      |
| `ItemStarted(FileChange)`           | Convert supplied changes and append the complete summary immediately; ignore the item's status and ID.                                                                         |
| Completion `Completed`              | Append nothing; retain the start summary unchanged.                                                                                                                            |
| Completion `Failed`                 | Retain the summary; append a separate failure cell with empty diagnostic input.                                                                                                |
| Completion `Declined`               | Append nothing; no declined title or restyling.                                                                                                                                |
| `FileChangeOutputDelta`             | No-op.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FileChangePatchUpdated`            | Ignored by notification dispatch; does not revise the stored diff.                                                                                                             |
| Turn interruption/abort             | No patch-specific transition. The already-committed summary persists; any interruption notice belongs to turn handling.                                                        |
| Thread-item replay, `InProgress`    | Ignore the file-change item; do not synthesize its start summary.                                                                                                              |
| Thread-item replay, terminal status | Run completion handling only: `Failed` adds the failure title; `Completed`/`Declined` add no patch cell. Buffered **start notifications** still take the ordinary start route. |

Sources: [start dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol.rs#L378-L403), [completion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L154-L165), [status enum](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/app-server-protocol/src/protocol/v2/item.rs#L1140-L1148), [output no-op](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol_requests.rs#L68-L68), [ignored update](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol.rs#L250-L257), [replay](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L195-L201), [interruption](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/input_restore.rs#L235-L257), [committed approval/start/success assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L1667-L1744).

Completion can be deferred behind an interrupt; when handled, every status reaching the completion helper sets `had_work_activity = true`, including failure/decline. There is no `Aborted` patch-status variant. [Deferral](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L47-L53), [completion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L154-L165).

Failure rendering is exactly one span, `"✘ Failed to apply patch"` (`✘` U+2718), foreground `Magenta`, background unset, modifier `BOLD`. The optional helper-only diagnostic path runs only when `stderr.trim()` is nonempty; it does **not** trim the supplied content. It constructs finalized `CommandOutput(exit_code=1)` and retains the first five and last five logical lines without overlap. If `N > 10`, insert the unprefixed DIM line `"… +{N-10} lines (ctrl + t to view transcript)"` between them. First diagnostic prefix is `"  └ "`; other prefixes are `"    "`. ANSI-derived foreground/background/modifiers survive, with `DIM` added to every span, including prefixes; tabs become four spaces. This is up to eleven diagnostic logical lines, not a five-row preview. `PlainHistoryCell` supplies the same retained content to transcript mode, so its generic transcript hint does not reveal omitted stderr. The live completion route supplies `String::new()`, hence normally only the title appears. [Failure constructor](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/patches.rs#L38-L57), [retention algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L103-L175), [ellipsis](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L246-L253), [hint literal](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/ui_consts.rs#L12-L12), [ANSI/tab helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/ansi-escape/src/lib.rs#L6-L37), [plain cell](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/base.rs#L16-L24).

### Projections and actual background composition

| Projection           | Patch representation                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Main rich history    | `create_diff_summary(changes, cwd, width)`; all file changes/hunks, with no patch row cap.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Ctrl+T transcript    | Same logical renderer at transcript width; no expanded patch variant.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Raw history          | Render the same summary at **10,000 columns**, concatenate each line's span contents, then create unstyled lines. Preserve headings, counts, line numbers, signs, tabs expanded to spaces, and renderer-inserted wraps. This is not original unified-diff source.                                              |
| Full-screen approval | Separate `DiffSummary` renderable under title `"P A T C H"`: sorted files, `"{path} (+A -D)"`, blank line, body inset **two** columns; blank separator between files. No bullet, verb, aggregate totals, branch prefix, rename arrow, or path-based syntax highlighting. Headers display the source path only. |

Sources: [patch projections](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/patches.rs#L12-L23), [raw width](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L102-L103), [style stripping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L163-L176), [transcript default](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L235-L251), [approval body](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L309-L348), [approval title](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/event_dispatch.rs#L2905-L2915).

A critical projection difference occurs **after** diff-line construction:

- At rich diff color depths, each changed `Line` carries a background; the four-space history prefix inherits it because `prefix_lines` preserves `line.style`. ANSI16 has no line background, even when the syntax theme supplies inserted/deleted backgrounds. No padding-to-width string is appended. [Line construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L906-L943), [prefix helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/line_utils.rs#L57-L75).
- Finalized terminal scrollback sets the line background, clears to end-of-line, and merges styles as **`span.style.patch(line.style)`**. Consequently the line background overrides the light gutter's distinct span background. The initial physical row is explicitly cleared under the line tint. Terminal-autowrapped continuation rows are cleared **before** the current line colors are set; the writer does not explicitly clear every overflow row under that tint. Normally the diff's hard wrapping avoids overflow, but minimum-one-column content capacity and forced-character output can exceed very narrow widths. This describes emitted command ordering, not an emulator-independent overflow screenshot. Standard prewrapping also applies line style over spans. [Scrollback writer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/insert_history.rs#L293-L340), [prewrap composition](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L884-L933).
- Transcript rendering passes the original lines to `HyperlinkParagraph` with default widget style; there is no patch selection/reversal overlay. Ratatui instead lets span styles override line styles, retaining the distinct light gutter. Dependency-derived detail: the locked `ratatui-widgets 0.3.2` `Paragraph::render_paragraph`/`render_line` styles emitted graphemes, not unused row-tail cells. Thus its blank tail is not the scrollback writer's end-of-line tint. This was checked against the checksum-matching dependency source, not inferred from the diff module's “full-width” comment. [Transcript caller](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay/scrolling.rs#L28-L42), [Paragraph adapter](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_hyperlinks/paragraph.rs#L21-L47), [locked widgets](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/Cargo.lock#L12531-L12548), [locked core style implementation dependency](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/Cargo.lock#L12455-L12475).

### File headers, counts, and paths

Input variants are `Add { content }`, `Delete { content }`, and `Update { unified_diff, move_path }`. The app-server adapter copies `FileUpdateChange.diff` directly into `content` for add/delete; only updates parse it as unified diff. [Model](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_model.rs#L8-L21), [adapter](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app_server_approval_conversions.rs#L28-L50).

Sort by the original `Path`'s Rust comparison, before display shortening; not by destination or displayed name. Add/delete counts are `content.lines().count()`. Update counts count parsed `Insert`/`Delete` records, excluding context and headers; parse failure yields `(0,0)`. Zero counts remain visible. Empty input maps render `"• Edited 0 files (+0 -0)"`. [Sorting/counts](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L369-L408), [parsed counts](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L774-L789).

| Part                                 | Exact text and span style                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| One-file heading                     | `"• "` DIM; `"Added"`, `"Deleted"`, or `"Edited"` BOLD; `" "`; path spans; `" "`; count spans.                                    |
| Multi-file/zero-file heading         | `"• "` DIM; `"Edited"` BOLD; `" {N} files "`; aggregate count spans.                                                              |
| Per-file heading, only when `N != 1` | `"  └ "` DIM; path spans; `" "`; file count spans. Every file uses `└` U+2514.                                                    |
| Path spans                           | Displayed original path, then optional **single span** `" → {destination}"` (`→` U+2192); no foreground, background, or modifier. |
| Count spans                          | `"("` default; `"+{A}"` Green; `" "` default; `"-{D}"` Red; `")"` default.                                                        |
| Separators                           | One empty default line between file chunks; none before first, none explicitly after last.                                        |
| Body inset                           | `"    "` default span prepended to every body line, including hunk separators and continuations.                                  |

Headers are not truncated or prewrapped by the diff renderer; their outer projection controls overflow. There is no `A`/`D`/`M` status column. [Exact assembly](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L400-L473).

Path formatting precedence: relative input unchanged; absolute descendant of cwd becomes its stripped suffix; otherwise, if cwd and file resolve to the same Git root, use `pathdiff::diff_paths(path,cwd)` or the original absolute path on failure; otherwise abbreviate a home-directory descendant with `~`, falling back to the absolute path. Source and destination are formatted independently. No quoting, escaping, ellipsis, or canonicalization is added here. [Path algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L751-L772), [home helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_command.rs#L35-L51).

### Hunks, numbering, whitespace, and wrapping

For add/delete, enumerate `content.lines()` from one, emitting only inserted/deleted rows. Empty content emits no body; a terminal newline does not create a phantom extra row. Updates use `diffy::Patch::from_str`; invalid input emits **no body and no parse-error notice**. Render all parsed hunks in supplied order, without `---`, `+++`, `@@`, hunk captions, or “No newline at end of file” markers. [Add/delete](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L490-L556), [update traversal](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L557-L746).

Initialize `old` and `new` from each hunk's old/new start. Insert: display `new`, increment `new`. Delete: display `old`, increment `old`. Context: display `new`, increment both. Compute one number-column width `G` for the whole file: decimal digit count of the largest **displayed** number, minimum one; add/delete use total content-line count. Between hunks emit `SP(G+1)` with context-gutter style, then `"⋮"` (U+22EE) DIM. With the outer inset, `⋮` occupies the ordinary sign column. No omitted-line count is computed. [Number prepass/separator](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L558-L613), [counter updates](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L630-L741), [digit width](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1035-L1041).

For history width `W`, set body width `B = max(W-4,0)` and content capacity `C = max(B-G-2,1)`. Each source row becomes:

1. First physical row: `SP(4)`, right-aligned decimal number plus `" "` as one gutter span, one sign span (`"+"`, `"-"`, or `" "`), content chunks. **No extra space follows the sign.**
2. Continuations: `SP(4)`, one gutter span containing `SP(G+2)`, content chunks. No repeated number/sign. The former sign column receives gutter styling, including its light-background tint.

[Widths and span construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L848-L943).

`wrap_styled_spans` hard-wraps across span boundaries, not at words. Preserve each originating span's style. Walk Unicode scalar values with `UnicodeWidthChar::width`; unknown widths count zero except tab, which counts four. Accumulate fitting characters; replace each tab with exactly `"    "`, independent of column/tab stops. Flush immediately at capacity, including when a span ends there. If the next character cannot fit, flush any accumulated row, then consume that character onto the fresh row even if it exceeds `C`; the forced branch uses unknown-width fallback one, or four for tab. Always return at least one chunk for empty content. Consequently tiny widths can overflow; this is not an unconditional width bound, nor grapheme-cluster-aware wrapping. [Algorithm and tab constants](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L50-L53), [wrapping implementation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L961-L1033).

The diff wrapper preserves ordinary whitespace and adds no visible whitespace markers, trailing-space warning, intraline changed-word emphasis, or strikethrough. Plain update rows remove trailing `\n` only; add/delete use Rust `lines()` (including CRLF handling). Syntax spans strip trailing `\n`/`\r` per token. Thus plain update content can retain `\r` that highlighted content removes. These are renderer-level properties: ordinary scrollback prewrapping subsequently uses `wrap_ranges_trim`, which can remove trailing spaces; transcript uses `Wrap { trim: false }`. [Plain update trimming](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L635-L704), [highlight trimming](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L647-L659), [scrollback wrapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/insert_history.rs#L232-L267), [trimming helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L274-L313).

### Terminal detection and exact background palettes

Resolve environment/style context once per `render_change` call. `Light` iff a sampled background exists and `0.299*r + 0.587*g + 0.114*b > 128.0` using `f32`; equality and unavailable samples select `Dark`. This is weighted sRGB intensity, not gamma-correct luminance. [Context](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L217-L227), [theme selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1044-L1056), [threshold](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/color.rs#L1-L5).

`default_bg()` reads the cached default-color pair. Unix/Windows caches record unsuccessful attempts too; startup-probe results seed the cache. The Unix standalone probe sends `"\u001b]10;?\u001b\\\u001b]11;?\u001b\\"`, requires both foreground and background replies, and uses a shared **100 ms** timeout. Missing responses/I/O errors become unavailable colors. Replies accept BEL or ESC-backslash termination, case-insensitive `rgb:`/`rgba:`, 1–4 hex digits per component, scaling with integer `value*255/(16^digits-1)`; RGBA alpha is parsed then ignored. [Cache/default background](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_palette.rs#L130-L285), [timeout](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_probe.rs#L24-L31), [probe](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_probe.rs#L242-L255), [parsing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_probe.rs#L719-L776).

Windows first tries the same OSC pair, excluding bracketed-paste lookalikes and restoring unrelated console input. Missing/invalid stdout returns unavailable colors; failed OSC probing falls back to `GetConsoleScreenBufferInfoEx`. Select foreground index `attributes & 0x0f`, background index `(attributes >> 4) & 0x0f`; decode each `ColorTable` entry as `(value & 255, (value >> 8) & 255, (value >> 16) & 255)`. Ignore `COMMON_LVB_REVERSE_VIDEO` when choosing defaults. [Windows preference/fallback](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_probe/windows.rs#L30-L75), [pair parsing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_probe/windows_replay.rs#L61-L80), [console decoding](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_probe/windows.rs#L294-L326).

Diff color depth uses this ordered decision table. `FORCE_COLOR` means **presence**, regardless of value:

| Condition                                                | Result                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| `WT_SESSION` present, `FORCE_COLOR` absent               | Truecolor, regardless of reported depth/name. |
| Reported ANSI16, detected `WindowsTerminal`, no override | Truecolor.                                    |
| Otherwise reported truecolor / ANSI256                   | Same depth.                                   |
| Otherwise reported ANSI16 / Unknown                      | ANSI16; not an unstyled/no-color mode.        |

The reported depth comes from cached `supports_color` stdout detection. [Detection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_palette.rs#L6-L20), [diff promotion policy](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1066-L1129).

Fallback palette (`I(n)` is `Color::Indexed(n)`):

| Theme/depth     | Add line background        | Delete line background     | Add/delete number-gutter backgrounds                    | Changed-number foreground |
| --------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------- |
| Dark truecolor  | `(33,58,43)` `#213A2B`     | `(74,34,29)` `#4A221D`     | unset / unset                                           | unset, DIM                |
| Light truecolor | `(218,251,225)` `#DAFBE1`  | `(255,235,233)` `#FFEBE9`  | `(172,238,187)` / `(255,206,203)`                       | `(31,35,40)`              |
| Dark ANSI256    | `I(22)` = `(0,95,0)`       | `I(52)` = `(95,0,0)`       | unset / unset                                           | unset, DIM                |
| Light ANSI256   | `I(194)` = `(215,255,215)` | `I(224)` = `(255,215,215)` | `I(157)` = `(175,255,175)` / `I(217)` = `(255,175,175)` | `I(236)` = `(48,48,48)`   |
| Dark ANSI16     | unset                      | unset                      | unset / unset                                           | unset, DIM                |
| Light ANSI16    | unset                      | unset                      | unset / unset                                           | `Black`, no DIM           |

These are constants, **not alpha blends** with the terminal background. The introductory comment's old dark hex values are not the executable palette. [Constants](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L55-L78), [palette/gutter selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1169-L1232), [Xterm table](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_palette.rs#L308-L568).

On rich depths, independently override add/delete line background from active-theme `markup.inserted`/`markup.deleted`, falling back to `diff.inserted`/`diff.deleted`. Query each scope with `Highlighter::style_mod_for_stack([scope])`; only a matching background counts, not the theme's general background. Extract `(r,g,b)` **ignoring alpha**. Truecolor preserves that tuple; ANSI256 quantizes it. The fixed light gutter backgrounds do not follow scope overrides. ANSI16 ignores all scope backgrounds. [Scope extraction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L294-L315), [resolution](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L234-L275).

ANSI256 scope quantization searches Xterm indices **16–255**, selects minimum CIE76 distance (first index on ties), and has index 22 as unreachable-empty-table fallback. Exact `f32` calculation: normalize each sRGB channel by 255; linearize with `c/12.92` when `c<=0.04045`, otherwise `((c+0.055)/1.055)^2.4`. XYZ matrix rows are `(0.4124,0.3576,0.1805)`, `(0.2126,0.7152,0.0722)`, `(0.0193,0.1192,0.9505)`; divide XYZ by D65 `(0.95047,1,1.08883)`. Set `f(t)=t^(1/3)` for `t>0.008856`, otherwise `7.787*t+16/116`. Lab is `(116*f(y)-16,500*(f(x)-f(y)),200*(f(y)-f(z)))`; distance is Euclidean. [Search](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L278-L296), [conversion constants](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/color.rs#L14-L75).

### Exact region styles and syntax exceptions

Let `A`/`D` be resolved backgrounds above; `K` means Green for insert, Red for delete. Entries below are **emitted styles before projection merging**.

| Region                            | Foreground                        | Background                        | Added modifiers                       |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| Insert/delete `Line`              | unset                             | `A`/`D`, or unset in ANSI16       | none                                  |
| Context `Line`                    | unset                             | unset                             | none                                  |
| Changed gutter, dark              | unset                             | unset                             | DIM                                   |
| Changed gutter, light             | Palette table's number foreground | Palette table's number background | none                                  |
| Context gutter, every depth/theme | unset                             | unset                             | DIM                                   |
| Changed sign, dark rich           | `K`                               | `A`/`D`                           | none                                  |
| Changed sign, light or ANSI16     | `K`                               | unset                             | none                                  |
| Context sign                      | unset                             | unset                             | none                                  |
| Plain changed content, dark rich  | `K`                               | `A`/`D`                           | none                                  |
| Plain changed content, light rich | unset                             | `A`/`D`                           | none                                  |
| Plain changed content, ANSI16     | `K`                               | unset                             | none                                  |
| Plain context content             | unset                             | unset                             | none                                  |
| Syntax insert/context content     | Converted syntax foreground       | unset                             | Syntax BOLD, if present               |
| Syntax delete content             | Converted syntax foreground       | unset                             | Syntax BOLD, if present, **plus DIM** |

Plain deletion content is **not** dimmed. Insert syntax is not forced green; deletion syntax is not forced red. Empty content creates a gutter/sign row but no content span after wrapping. All diff-generated removed-modifier sets are empty. No region introduces italic, underline, reverse, or crossed-out. The otherwise-unused “rich depth but no resolved background” branch gives light plain content default style and dark plain content `K` only. [Line layer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1153-L1167), [gutter/sign/content styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1212-L1317), [syntax overlay](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L883-L925).

Syntax foreground conversion is independent of diff color depth: syntect alpha `0` means ANSI index in its red byte (0–7 map to Black, Red, Green, Yellow, Blue, Magenta, Cyan, Gray; 8–255 remain Indexed); alpha `1` means foreground unset; all other alpha values produce RGB directly. Syntax backgrounds, italic, and underline are discarded; BOLD is retained.
