Therefore ANSI16's “foreground-only” guarantee does **not** mean syntax tokens are quantized to sixteen colors: an RGB syntax theme can still produce RGB foreground spans. [Conversion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L468-L533).

Only the path extension selects syntax; renames use the destination extension, even for deleted lines. No extension means no highlighting, including extensionless `Makefile`. Add/delete highlight the complete supplied content; updates concatenate all context/delete/insert payloads **in diff order** per hunk, without inserted separators. Parser state persists within that combined hunk, not separately on each diff side or across hunks. If highlighted-line count differs from hunk-record count, discard highlighting for the whole hunk. [Selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L462-L494), [hunk highlighting](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L589-L629).

Highlighting rejects empty input, unknown syntax, parse errors, more than **524,288 bytes**, more than **10,000 lines**, or any line longer than **4,096 bytes**; equality is permitted. Updates additionally precheck aggregate hunk-payload bytes/record count, including payload newlines but excluding diff headers/signs. Guards disable syntax work, never diff rendering or row output. [Guard constants/core](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L583-L642), [aggregate precheck](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L558-L597), [10,001-line assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L2444-L2491).

### Worked examples and verification boundaries

**Single replacement**, source `"line one\nline two\nline three\n"`, replacement `"line one\nline two changed\nline three\n"`, `example.txt`, width 80. Exact logical rows, without snapshot framing/right padding:

```json
[
  "• Edited example.txt (+1 -1)",
  "    1  line one",
  "    2 -line two",
  "    2 +line two changed",
  "    3  line three"
]
```

The duplicate number 2 is deliberate: old-side deletion then new-side insertion. Header counts are Green/Red regardless of theme. With no syntax and dark truecolor fallback, deleted text/sign are Red on `(74,34,29)` and inserted text/sign Green on `(33,58,43)`; context text/sign are default, number gutters DIM. [Committed input](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1535-L1558), [text snapshot](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/snapshots/codex_tui__diff_render__tests__apply_update_block.snap#L5-L9), [style implementation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1237-L1317).

**Wrapped light insertion**, matching the committed helper test: number 12, `G=2`, body width 8, content `"abcdefghij"`, no syntax. Capacity is four; inner rows are `"12 +abcd"`, `"    efgh"`, `"    ij"`. First gutter span is `"12 "`; continuation gutter span has four spaces and therefore covers the sign position too. Light truecolor fallback gives each line `(218,251,225)` background and each gutter `(31,35,40)` foreground on `(172,238,187)`; sign foreground is Green; content foreground unset. In history add four spaces to each row (total width parameter 12). The transcript preserves the gutter's stronger tint; finalized scrollback overrides it with the line tint as described above. [Committed style assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L2083-L2115).

**Tabs and scope override:** committed wrapping assertions establish `"\tabcde"` at capacity eight → `"    abcd"`, `"e"`; `"abcd\t界"` at capacity five → `"abcd"`, `"    "`, `"界"`. A theme insert background `(0,95,0)` quantizes to index 22 in ANSI256; missing delete scope retains fallback index 52. ANSI16 ignores both supplied backgrounds. [Wrapping assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L2379-L2422), [quantization assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1957-L1975), [ANSI16 assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L1995-L2043).

**Sorting, rename, and separated hunks**, algorithm-derived example at width 80: supply `b.txt` added with `"tail\n"`, plus `a.old` updated/moved to `a.txt` with diff `"--- a.old\n+++ a.txt\n@@ -2 +2 @@\n-old\n+new\n@@ -10 +10 @@\n-last\n+final\n"`. The renderer produces:

```json
[
  "• Edited 2 files (+3 -2)",
  "  └ a.old → a.txt (+2 -2)",
  "     2 -old",
  "     2 +new",
  "       ⋮",
  "    10 -last",
  "    10 +final",
  "",
  "  └ b.txt (+1 -0)",
  "    1 +tail"
]
```

`a.old` precedes `b.txt` by original-path ordering; its `G=2` affects both hunks, while `b.txt` independently uses `G=1`. The ellipsis is DIM/default-background, not an omission budget. [Header/sorting algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L369-L473), [hunk/number algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L558-L613).

Terminal palette RGBs and active/custom syntax-theme token colors are environment-dependent parameters. The fallback palette, scope overrides, and style composition rules above specify how those parameters affect the diff.

## Other tool families

### Notation and projection rules

This chapter describes Codex at commit `121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df`, not proposed Pi behavior. Quoted strings preserve spaces; `"    "` means four ASCII spaces. Style notation names Ratatui foregrounds and modifiers: **default** means no foreground/background/modifier override, not white. Backgrounds are unset unless explicitly described. `PlainHistoryCell` returns its stored logical lines without width-dependent wrapping; raw mode concatenates spans and removes styles **without removing glyphs or prefixes**. Transcript defaults to the rich display projection. Viewport wrapping still applies to oversized logical lines. [`base.rs` lines 6–24](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/base.rs#L6-L24), [`HistoryCell` defaults](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L163-L264).

`PrefixedWrappedHistoryCell` adaptively wraps at the supplied width, returns no lines at width zero, and omits its prefixes/styles in raw mode. Adaptive wrapping preserves URL-like tokens as logical units, including over-width URLs; it is not a strict clipping algorithm. `raw_lines_from_source` splits on LF and removes exactly one terminal empty component. In contrast, fresh `Line::from(String)` construction concatenates its `str::lines()` fragments into adjacent spans on one line, removing embedded LF/CRLF separators; see the shared string-to-line contract. This also applies when `plain_lines` rebuilds style-stripped lines. [`prefix wrapper`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/base.rs#L61-L96), [`adaptive wrapping`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L683-L763), [`raw source splitting`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L147-L161).

### Web search

| Input/state   | Rich logical content and styles                                                                           |
| ------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Pending       | Activity indicator; default `" "`; bold `"Searching the web"`; if detail nonempty, default `" " + detail` |
| Completed     | Dim `"•"`; default `" "`; bold `"Searched the web"`; if detail nonempty, default `" for " + detail`       |
| Failure/abort | No search-specific failure label, error body, duration, or result count                                   |

All rows use initial prefix `[indicator, " "]`, continuation `"  "`. Raw is one default line with the same header/detail separator but no bullet; embedded LF/CRLF in its formatted detail disappear through `Line::from(String)`. Transcript does not expand search results. [`search renderer`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/search.rs#L5-L46), [`projections`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/search.rs#L90-L123).

Detail selection is exact, with no trimming:

1. `Search`: use nonempty `action.query`; otherwise first `action.queries` element. Append ASCII `" ..."` only when there are multiple queries **and the first is nonempty**. Do not search later entries for a nonempty query.
2. `OpenPage`: URL or `""`.
3. `FindInPage`: pattern+URL → `"'{pattern}' in {url}"`; pattern only → `"'{pattern}'"`; URL only → URL; neither → `""`. `Some("")` still participates in formatting.
4. `Other`/absent action → `""`. If the resulting detail is empty, use the item's top-level query. [`algorithm`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/search.rs#L13-L46).

Begin flushes preceding content and installs an empty-query active cell. End updates/completes a matching call ID and commits it; without a match it inserts a separately completed cell. Shared completed-item/replay handling calls begin then end, substituting `Other` for absent action; that adapter can therefore commit an earlier pending search unchanged before adding the completed row. Abort finalization marks only exec/MCP cells failed: an unfinished search is committed unchanged, potentially still reading `Searching the web`. [`lifecycle`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L71-L109), [`replay`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L207-L215), [`abort exception`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget.rs#L1390-L1402).

The indicator is a dim static `"•"` with animations disabled. Animated non-truecolor alternates default `"•"` and dim `"◦"` every 600 ms; truecolor uses the shared bold RGB shimmer on `"•"`. Search does not override `transcript_animation_tick`. [`motion`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/motion.rs#L38-L78), [`shimmer styles`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/shimmer.rs#L21-L79), [`complete search implementation`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/search.rs#L90-L123).

At width 64 the committed assertion expects these exact lines; the styles come from the renderer, not the assertion:

```text
• Searched the web for example search query with several generic
  words to exercise wrapping
```

[`worked example assertion`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L1274-L1293).

### Checklist updates and proposed plans

**Checklist update:** each `TurnPlanUpdated` inserts a new cell; this is not an in-place pending/success tool card. Heading: dim `"• "`, bold `"Updated Plan"`. Trim explanation at both ends, suppress if empty, otherwise dim+italic. Empty plan: dim+italic `"(no steps provided)"`. Body uses first prefix dim `"  └ "`, later prefix default `"    "`. Explanation wraps at `max(width−4,1)`; each step wraps at that same width including its checkbox indent. [`update lifecycle`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/turn_runtime.rs#L531-L545), [`renderer`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/plans.rs#L175-L226).

| Step status | Initial indent | Step text                   | Wrapped continuation |
| ----------- | -------------- | --------------------------- | -------------------- |
| Completed   | Default `"✔ "` | Default FG, dim+crossed-out | Default `"  "`       |
| InProgress  | Default `"□ "` | Cyan+bold                   | Default `"  "`       |
| Pending     | Default `"□ "` | Default FG, dim             | Default `"  "`       |

The checkbox is **not** styled with its step text. Raw emits `Updated Plan`, trimmed explanation lines, then `Completed: {step}`, `InProgress: {step}`, or `Pending: {step}`; empty-plan fallback remains. No list numbering, percentage, timestamps, or failure state. [`step/raw implementation`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/plans.rs#L186-L247). For `InProgress("Define error taxonomy")`, then `Pending("Implement mapping to user messages")`, width 40 yields the first step under `"  └ □ "`, second under `"    □ "`; continuation text starts after six spaces. Tests also assert that long URL-like tokens remain intact. [`fixtures and assertions`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L2544-L2600).

**Proposed plan:** finalized cell retains Markdown and cwd. Rich/transcript sequence: heading `[dim "• ", bold "Proposed Plan"]`; default `" "` spacer; background-styled `" "` top padding; Markdown rendered at `max(width−4,1)` with default `"  "` prefixes; background-styled `" "` bottom padding. Empty rendered body becomes dim+italic `"(empty)"`. Raw returns Markdown only. No truncation or checklist status conversion occurs. Markdown span styles remain those of the shared Markdown renderer. [`final plan`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/plans.rs#L103-L141).

Plan background equals user-message background: known light background (`0.299r+0.587g+0.114b >128`) blends 4% black; otherwise blends 12% white; channels truncate to `u8`. Unknown background adds no style. `best_color` uses RGB in truecolor, nearest fixed xterm color by perceptual distance in 256-color, reset in ANSI16/unknown. It does not override foreground or modifiers. [`plan/background styles`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/style.rs#L80-L137), [`blend`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/color.rs#L1-L12), [`color selection`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_palette.rs#L77-L89).

Plan deltas render only in Plan mode. Stream fragments emit heading/top padding once, bottom padding only on finalize; unlike finalized padding, streamed padding is itself prefixed with `"  "`. Transient cells retain rendered lines, so their raw projection strips styles but retains framing. Completion may consolidate streamed source; it does **not universally replace it with completed-item text**: the branch with a final streamed cell explicitly retains controller source. Abort drops preview tail/controller without inventing plan failure/checkmarks. [`delta/completion branches`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/streaming.rs#L145-L228), [`stream framing`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/streaming/controller.rs#L707-L757), [`transient raw`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/plans.rs#L147-L168), [`termination`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/turn_runtime.rs#L324-L358).

### Image viewing and generation

All image notices are `PlainHistoryCell`s: no thumbnail, family-specific clipping, special transcript expansion, or raw payload recovery. [`image constructors`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/patches.rs#L60-L96).

| Family/input                          | Exact span sequence                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Image view completion                 | `[dim "• ", bold "Viewed Image"]`; `[dim "  └ ", dim display_path]`                            |
| Generation, status exactly `"failed"` | `[red+bold "✗ ", bold "Image generation failed"]`                                              |
| Generation, every other status        | `[dim "• ", bold "Generated Image:"]`                                                          |
| Generation detail                     | `[dim "  └ ", dim revised_prompt.unwrap_or(call_id)]`; a present empty prompt stays empty      |
| Optional generation saved path        | `[dim "  └ ", dim "Saved to: ", default file_URI]`; URI conversion failure uses displayed path |

Viewing converts the legacy path to inferred URI then host absolute path; failure preserves the original string. `display_path_for`: relative path unchanged; descendant of cwd → cwd-relative; same Git root → relative path; otherwise home-relative `~` when possible, else absolute. [`path algorithm`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs#L748-L772). Generation begin only flushes answer text and restores the global status indicator if running. Completed/replayed items insert notices; no image-specific pending/abort card exists. Image validation errors occur before the core emits `ImageView`, so they do not produce `Viewed Image`. [`lifecycle`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L14-L45), [`replay`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L216-L226), [`validation/emission`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/core/src/tools/handlers/view_image.rs#L160-L211).

Committed assertion: completed generation with prompt `A tiny blue square` and `/tmp/generated-image.png` produces `"• Generated Image:"`, `"  └ A tiny blue square"`, `"  └ Saved to: file:///tmp/generated-image.png"` on Unix. [`assertion`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/tests.rs#L518-L539).

### Collaboration and dynamic-tool visibility

Collaboration notices are plain stored lines, not mutable tool cards. Common title prefix is dim `"• "`; action text is bold; details use first dim `"  └ "`, later default `"    "`. Labels trim nickname/role: nickname cyan+bold, otherwise thread UUID cyan, otherwise literal cyan `"agent"`; role adds dim `" "` then default `"[{role}]"`. Optional spawn suffix adds dim `" "` then magenta `"({model} {effort})"` or `"({effort})"`; omit when trimmed model empty and effort is default `medium`. Effort text is the wire value (`none`, `minimal`, `low`, `medium`, `high`, `xhigh`, `max`, `ultra`, `persistent`, or custom string). [`effort values`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/protocol/src/openai_models.rs#L56-L94). Both model and effort must be supplied to construct the summary, cached by call ID across start/end. [`title/label rules`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L468-L555), [`spawn cache`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L117-L147), [`required fields`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L188-L201).

| Tool                                                  | InProgress                                                                                                                   | Any terminal tool status                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| SpawnAgent                                            | No cell                                                                                                                      | `"Spawned " + label + suffix`, or `"Agent spawn failed"` if first receiver missing/invalid; optional prompt detail |
| SendInput                                             | No cell                                                                                                                      | `"Sent input to " + label`; optional prompt; missing/invalid first receiver → no cell                              |
| ResumeAgent                                           | `"Resuming " + label`                                                                                                        | `"Resumed " + label`; status detail, fallback `"Error - Agent resume failed"`                                      |
| Wait                                                  | One valid receiver: `"Waiting for " + label`; zero: `"Waiting for agents"`; multiple: `"Waiting for {n} agents"` plus labels | `"Finished waiting"` plus available status rows; none → default `"No agents completed yet"`                        |
| CloseAgent                                            | No cell                                                                                                                      | `"Closed " + label`; missing/invalid first receiver → no cell                                                      |
| SendMessage, FollowupTask, InterruptAgent, ListAgents | No cell                                                                                                                      | No cell: analytics-only variants                                                                                   |

Resume also requires a valid first receiver. Terminal `Failed` is not generally mapped to a red title: spawn success depends on receiver presence; close/send do not inspect agent state. Wait begin/end and resume begin/end append separate notices. [`dispatch truth table`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L203-L284), [`titles/details`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L343-L466).

| Agent status | Status spans                                                                                                                |
| ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| PendingInit  | Cyan `"Pending init"`                                                                                                       |
| Running      | Cyan+bold `"Running"`                                                                                                       |
| Interrupted  | Yellow `"Interrupted"`                                                                                                      |
| Completed    | Green `"Completed"`; nonempty normalized message adds dim `" - "`, default preview                                          |
| Errored      | Red `"Error"`; nonempty normalized message adds dim `" - "`, default preview; missing message defaults to `"Agent errored"` |
| Shutdown     | Default `"Shutdown"`                                                                                                        |
| NotFound     | Red `"Not found"`                                                                                                           |

Prompt trims outer whitespace but preserves internal whitespace, limited to **160 extended graphemes**. Completed/error messages first collapse `split_whitespace()` with `" "`, then limit to **240/160** graphemes. Overflow keeps `limit−3` graphemes plus ASCII `"..."`. Wait rows are `label + dim ": " + status`: receiver order first when state exists, then extra valid state UUIDs sorted lexically. Duplicate receivers are not deduplicated in that first pass. Resume chooses first receiver with a state, else lexically smallest state-map key. Raw removes styles only: omitted prompt/message content is not restored. [`status/order algorithms`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L557-L674), [`limits`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L29-L31), [`truncator`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/text_formatting.rs#L88-L114).

For the committed Robie example, title spans produce `"• Spawned Robie [explorer] (gpt-5 high)"`. Tests directly assert cyan+bold nickname, default non-dim role, and magenta suffix. [`style assertions`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L892-L927).

`SubAgentActivity` instead uses bold `"Started "`, `"Interacted with "`, `"Interrupted "`, or `"Completed "`, followed by cyan path **including literal backticks**, with no details. Started sets running hint true; Interrupted/Completed false; Interacted leaves liveness unchanged. [`activity mapping`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L286-L341). Picker names are `Main [default]`, trimmed `nickname [role]`, nickname, `[role]`, or `Agent`; its dot is green unless closed, then default. [`picker helpers`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/multi_agents.rs#L75-L103).

**Dynamic calls:** started items fall through and shared completed/replay `DynamicToolCall` does nothing, regardless of success/error. Server-request execution returns a tool response, not a generic history card. Exceptions: delegated `FunctionCallOutput` from namespace `codex_tui`/`codex_app`, tool `create_thread`/`send_message_to_thread`, with the exact delegation envelope, renders default `"Sent by Codex from task {source_thread_id}\n{prompt}"` under dim `"• "`/default `"  "`. [`item visibility`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol.rs#L399-L435), [`completion exception`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L237-L283), [`envelope parser`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/dynamic_tools.rs#L1176-L1200), [`execution response`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/app_server_events.rs#L395-L481).

The `/subagents` status history is another visibility exception: magenta `/subagents`, bold `Sub-agents running`, blank line; empty → italic `"  • No sub-agents running."`. Each entry has dim `"  • "` + cyan backticked path; empty preview → default `"    "` + dim+italic `"No recent activity yet."`. Scan buffered starts/completions newest-first, deduplicate item IDs, retain six summaries, reverse; truncate each to 240 graphemes **before** whitespace collapse; wrap at `max(width−4,1)`, retain last three nonblank lines, dim body with four-space indent. Dynamic summary is `"Tool {namespace}/{tool}"` or `"Tool {tool}"`, with no status/result; raw recomputes at `u16::MAX`, retaining these limits. [`status projection`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/agent_status_feed.rs#L16-L130), [`summary mapping`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/agent_status_feed.rs#L133-L213).

### Background terminal waiting and stdin

| Event                                                                                 | Visible/state effect                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Any interaction while task not running                                                | Suppressed                                                                                                                                                |
| Empty stdin, unknown process                                                          | Suppressed                                                                                                                                                |
| Empty stdin, known process                                                            | Global `"Waiting for background terminal"`; command detail, capitalization preserved, one-line budget; repeated same-process polls update one wait streak |
| Poll switches process                                                                 | Commit previous streak; start new streak; the flush restores reasoning/`Working` after the earlier waiting-status assignment                              |
| Nonempty stdin                                                                        | Commit same-process wait streak, then insert interaction notice; unknown command allowed                                                                  |
| Matching process completion / normal turn completion / preparation for assistant text | Flush wait streak                                                                                                                                         |
| Abort finalization                                                                    | Clear unflushed streak without inserting a wait notice; already committed notices remain                                                                  |

[`interaction state machine`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L9-L17), [`event branches`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L75-L156), [`turn completion`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/turn_runtime.rs#L138-L154), [`assistant boundary`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L9-L14), [`abort clearing`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/turn_runtime.rs#L324-L349). The misleadingly named `interrupt_preserves_unified_exec_wait_streak` snapshot contains only the interruption error, not a wait notice. [`committed snapshot`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/snapshots/codex_tui__chatwidget__tests__interrupt_preserves_unified_exec_wait_streak.snap#L1-L6).

Committed wait header is **one bold span** `"• Waited for background terminal"` (bullet also bold). Nonempty-input header is dim `"↳ "` then bold `"Interacted with background terminal"`. A nonempty known command appends dim `" · "`, dim command. Header adaptively wraps at full width without continuation indent. Stdin uses `.lines()`, default content, first dim `"  └ "`, subsequent dim `"    "`; no preview cap. Width zero returns nothing. Raw uses unprefixed `"Waited for background terminal: {command}"`/`"Interacted with background terminal: {command}"`, omits colon+command if absent/empty, then raw stdin lines. [`interaction renderer`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/exec.rs#L21-L96).

Status arbitration is observable: flushing a switched-process streak restores extracted reasoning heading or `Working` and clears details; active compaction without retry instead forces `Compacting context` / `Making room to continue.`. [`restore ordering`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/streaming.rs#L9-L19), [`status overrides`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/status_controls.rs#L14-L81), [`compaction literals`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/compaction.rs#L6-L7).

The live status row uses the shared activity indicator (hidden in reduced motion), shimmer/default header, then dim `"({elapsed} • "`, dim key hint, dim `" to interrupt)"`; default binding displays `esc`. Elapsed is seconds, `Mm SSs`, or `Hh MMm SSs`. Optional background summary appends dim `" · " + summary`. Header overflow keeps graphemes fitting `width−1`, then `"…"` in the last retained span's style. Detail is dim with `"  └ "`/`"    "`; leading whitespace trimmed, `break_words=true`; overflow retains one line and replaces its last span with up to `max(width−4,1)−1` **characters** plus `"…"` - this last step is not display-width/grapheme based. [`status rendering`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/status_indicator_widget.rs#L71-L103), [`detail algorithm`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/status_indicator_widget.rs#L116-L134), [`layout/styles`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/status_indicator_widget.rs#L171-L273), [`header truncation`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/line_truncation.rs#L75-L102).

Footer text is exactly `"{n} background terminal{s} running · /ps to view · /stop to close"`, singular when `n=1`; none for zero. Dedicated footer: prepend `"  "`, dim all, clip by display-width prefix without ellipsis; hidden below width 4. [`footer`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/unified_exec_footer.rs#L40-L67).

`/ps` inserts magenta `/ps`, blank separator, bold `Background terminals`, blank; empty → italic `"  • No background terminals running."`. Show at most 16 processes. Command: first source line, at most 80 graphemes, cyan after dim `"  • "`; multiline, grapheme overflow, or width overflow requests dim suffix `" [...]"`. Reserve suffix only when available width exceeds six cells, otherwise clip without it. Output preview prefixes are dim `"    ↳ "` then `"      "`, contents/suffix dim. Prefix alone is emitted when width cannot accommodate it. Remaining processes: dim `"  • ... and {n} more running"`, clipped without suffix. Retain latest three nonempty, right-trimmed output lines per process, decoding lossily. Raw still caps processes/commands/previews, rendering at `u16::MAX`. [`process renderer`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/exec.rs#L123-L243), [`retention`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/command_lifecycle.rs#L217-L240).

Two empty polls for `just fix` produce one waiting status, command detail `just fix`, and one flushed wait notice at turn completion; a finished/unknown process does not change `Working`. These are committed test assertions, not interactive observations. Buffered `TerminalInteraction` notifications use the same running/process guards; shared item-only replay does not synthesize these stdin/poll notices. [`notification dispatch`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/protocol.rs#L95-L97), [`item replay`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L185-L199), [`tests`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L989-L1031).

### Notices

| Constructor   | Rich strings/styles                                                                                      | Raw                                                                                 |
| ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Warning       | Yellow `"⚠ "` and yellow message; adaptive continuation default `"  "`                                   | Message only                                                                        |
| Info          | Dim `"• "`, default message; optional default `" "`, DarkGray hint                                       | Retains glyph/hint                                                                  |
| Error         | Entire `"■ {message}"` red                                                                               | Retains square and ASCII space; contrary to its comment, code inserts no hair space |
| Deprecation   | Red+bold `"⚠ "`, red summary; optional dim details adaptively wrapped at `max(width−4,1)`, **no indent** | Summary then source detail lines, no warning glyph                                  |
| Review status | Entire supplied message cyan                                                                             | Same text, no styles                                                                |

[`warning`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/notices.rs#L86-L89), [`deprecation/info/error`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/notices.rs#L203-L251), [`review status`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/approvals.rs#L345-L350).

## Tool interaction surfaces

### Reading the rendering contracts

This chapter describes Codex at `121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df`, not proposed Pi behavior. Quoted strings preserve spaces; `SP×n` means exactly _n_ ASCII spaces. Unless overridden below, spans have default foreground, no explicit background, and no modifiers. `DIM`, `BOLD`, and `ITALIC` name Ratatui modifiers; cyan/red/green mean the named ANSI colors, not fixed RGB values. These cells inherit `transcript_lines(width) = display_lines(width)`; raw history is a separately generated, unstyled projection, not an ANSI-stripped screenshot. Outer history measurement uses `Paragraph` with `Wrap { trim: false }`, including logical lines wider than the viewport; the shared default converts the measured height to `u16` with `.try_into().unwrap_or(0)`, reporting zero on overflow without removing content. [History contract](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L163-L265)

Both question and approval menus use a borderless surface, inset one row vertically and two columns horizontally. Its only explicit style is background: with a detected light background, blend 4% black into it; otherwise blend 12% white; unknown background leaves the style default. “Light” means `0.299r + 0.587g + 0.114b > 128`; blend components truncate to `u8`. Color conversion uses RGB for truecolor, nearest fixed xterm color by CIE76 distance for ANSI256, and terminal default for ANSI16/unknown. Selected menu spans **replace** their styles with `accent_style`: BOLD cyan on dark/unknown backgrounds, BOLD best-supported `(0,95,135)` on light backgrounds. Selection does not paint another background. [Surface](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/selection_popup_common.rs#L91-L120), [selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/selection_popup_common.rs#L329-L343), [styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/style.rs#L81-L132), [color arithmetic](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/color.rs#L1-L12), [conversion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_palette.rs#L77-L89)

### Interactive request lifecycle

Exec, patch, permissions, and user-input requests enter `defer_or_handle`: queue whenever an answer stream exists **or** an interrupt is already queued, preserving FIFO until stream completion flushes the queue. Immediate handlers flush the answer stream with its separator before presenting the modal. Pending exec approvals themselves produce **no proposed-command history cell**, explicitly asserted by the committed test. [Deferral](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/streaming.rs#L450-L475), [handlers](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_requests.rs#L267-L335), [user-input/permissions handlers](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_requests.rs#L420-L456), [assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/approval_requests.rs#L8-L50)

| Event/state                      | Exact interaction/history consequence                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Question request becomes current | Pause status timer; disable underlying input with `"Answer the questions to continue."`; initialize question 1 and fresh drafts. Further requests consumed by this overlay use FIFO `VecDeque`.                          |
| Question submitted               | Send `UserInputAnswer` using `turn_id`; insert `RequestUserInputResultCell(interrupted=false)`; advance queue or complete.                                                                                               |
| Nonblocking question expires     | Send an **empty map**, insert the same result-cell type with no answers, then advance. Not an error or interruption.                                                                                                     |
| Question interrupted             | Send interrupt and finish overlay; discard pending local exchange, including committed answers and queued prompts. **No answer response or interrupted result cell is emitted.**                                         |
| Request resolved elsewhere       | Prune matching queued request; if current, advance/dismiss without sending a decision or adding history. User-input identity is `item_id == call_id`; command/patch/permissions identity includes thread and request ID. |
| Approval selected                | Emit explicit request-specific decision; advance overlay's internal `Vec` using `pop()` (LIFO for requests appended while already open).                                                                                 |
| Approval canceled                | Cancel only current exec/patch/elicitation, or deny current permissions; clear queued approvals and finish. No separate decisions for cleared entries.                                                                   |

Sources: [question opening](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/mod.rs#L1682-L1709), [question submission/resolution](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L874-L971), [interrupt/paste/queue](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1471-L1542), [approval identity/resolution](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L141-L229), [selection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L324-L365), [cancel/queue](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L491-L535).

Approvals have a separate **one-second typing-idle delay**, reset by further composer activity. Delayed requests promote oldest-first; remaining delayed requests are enqueued in reverse order specifically to compensate for the modal's `pop()`. An already-open compatible overlay consumes a new request before this delay logic. Questions do not use this delay. [Constant](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/mod.rs#L190-L194), [promotion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/mod.rs#L669-L714), [routing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/mod.rs#L1644-L1709)

Replay is not a new visual state. Buffered requests survive thread refresh; matching resolved requests dismiss their overlays. Hook begin/end notifications also survive refresh. Patch approval reconstruction recovers changes by thread/turn/item before constructing the same modal/fullscreen diff. The question-result cell has only two production construction sites here - manual and automatic submission - both set `interrupted=false`; do not infer persisted interrupted exchanges from its rendering support. [Refresh](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/thread_events.rs#L59-L83), [patch recovery](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/file_change_approvals.rs#L9-L71), [question constructors](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L919-L952)

### Question modal: presentation, input, and layout

The overlay renders `question.question`, not the question's `header`. Initial option cursor is index 0 but **uncommitted**, so merely highlighting an option does not answer it. An options question is answered iff selection exists and `answer_committed`; a freeform question uses `answer_committed` alone. Nonempty submitted freeform text commits; empty submission does not. Editing drafts or moving the option cursor invalidates commitment. [Initialization](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L721-L750), [answer predicate](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1037-L1074), [submission](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1105-L1143)

| Component              | Literal text and span style                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Progress               | `"Question {i}/{total}"`, optionally `" ({n} unanswered)"`; all DIM. Empty request: `"No questions"`.                                                                                                                                                              |
| Auto-resolution suffix | DIM `" · "` then red `"auto-resolves in {remaining}"`, without DIM.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Question               | Default if answered; cyan if unanswered; no bold. Plain `textwrap::wrap` at `max(width,1)`.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Option                 | Selected prefix `"› {n}. "`; otherwise `"  {n}. "`. Label default, description DIM; selected row replaces **all** span styles, including description/padding, with accent/BOLD.                                                                                    |
| Synthetic other option | Only when `is_other` and options are nonempty: label `"None of the above"`; description `"Optionally, add details in notes (tab)."`.                                                                                                                               |
| Notes placeholder      | `"Add notes"`; if selection cleared, `"Select an option to add notes"`; freeform `"Type your answer (optional)"`. DIM.                                                                                                                                             |
| Notes input            | Shared plain-text composer: default text, BOLD `"›"` and one following gutter column; same surface background, top/bottom padding 1 and right padding 1. Secret input uses one `'*'` per display column of each visible grapheme, not the six-bullet history mask. |

Sources: [progress/options](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L337-L472), [constants](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L54-L70), [render styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L266-L299), [mask](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L410-L422), [composer layout](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/chat_composer.rs#L1051-L1060), [composer rendering](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/chat_composer.rs#L4946-L5032), [gutter](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/ui_consts.rs#L3-L10), [width-preserving mask](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/textarea.rs#L2217-L2242).

The nested composer disables popups, slash commands, shell commands, and image attachment/paste behavior; its own footer is explicitly empty. Cursor is exposed only with notes focus and a nonempty notes rectangle, never in confirmation. [Setup](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L203-L226), [plain-text preset](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/chat_composer.rs#L500-L515), [cursor](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L384-L407)

| Input                                             | Transition                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Options Up/Down, `k`/`j`, configured list up/down | Wrap selection; clear commitment. Space commits without advancing. Backspace/Delete clears selection **and** notes.                                                                                             |
| Options Enter/list accept                         | Commit and advance; on last question submit or open unanswered confirmation. Exception: synthetic other selection opens notes instead.                                                                          |
| Digits 1–9 matching an option                     | Select, commit, advance immediately; unlike Enter, the synthetic other option does **not** get the notes-opening exception. `0` is not an option shortcut.                                                      |
| Options Tab with selection                        | Open notes. Ordinary typing does **not** open notes, despite the module's introductory comment. Nonempty paste does.                                                                                            |
| Notes Tab or Esc with options                     | Destructively clear notes, clear commitment, hide notes, focus options. Empty-notes Backspace closes notes without clearing selection. Notes Up/Down changes option selection, not textarea line.               |
| Notes submit binding                              | Submit via composer; preserve pending-paste draft, then advance/submit. If composer returns no submission, options still commit; freeform does not gain commitment.                                             |
| Ctrl+P/PageUp; Ctrl+N/PageDown                    | Previous/next question, wrapping; save/restore per-question draft. Options focus also accepts Left/`h`, Right/`l`, configured list left/right. Composer submit takes precedence over these navigation bindings. |
| Interrupt binding                                 | Finish with interrupt, except Esc first clears visible option notes. Ctrl+C first clears nonempty notes; otherwise interrupts. In confirmation Ctrl+C interrupts.                                               |

These branches are implemented in [key handling](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1221-L1465), [Ctrl+C/paste](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1471-L1510); tests explicitly prove [digit submission and ignored ordinary typing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L2322-L2388).

**Submission encoding.** Emit one map entry per question. Only committed answers contribute their selected label and trimmed expanded notes; notes append `"user_note: {text}"`. Skipped/uncommitted questions have `answers: []`, not a `"skipped"` string. Final confirmation defaults to **Proceed**, not Go back: BOLD `"Submit with unanswered questions?"`; DIM `"{n} unanswered question[s]"`; rows `"› 1. Proceed"` / `"  2. Go back"`, descriptions `"Submit with {n} unanswered question[s]."` / `"Return to the first unanswered question."`. Enter activates; 1/2 only highlight; Up/Down/`k`/`j` wrap; Esc/Backspace return to first unanswered. These keys are hardcoded, not the configured list map. Footer is `"Press enter to confirm or esc to go back"`, with only the key spans DIM. [Encoding](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L874-L933), [confirmation state/text](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L974-L1035), [keys](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1145-L1185), [render](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L119-L174), [footer literals](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/popup_consts.rs#L15-L24), [key style](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/key_hint.rs#L224-L243)

**Timeout.** `is_blocking=true` disables it. Otherwise: 60 seconds hidden grace, 60 seconds visible countdown, then empty-map resolution; deprecated `autoResolutionMs` is ignored. Any non-release key entering `handle_key_event`, or nonempty paste, snoozes permanently for that request, even a key with no visible effect. The separate Ctrl+C callback clears nonempty notes without itself snoozing. Queue advancement restarts timing. Display seconds round up; below 60 use `"{s}s"`, otherwise `"{m}m {s:02}s"`. Schedule grace expiry, then at most one-second intervals. Committed assertions verify red countdown spans and the empty response. [Algorithm](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L68-L97), [timing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L269-L342), [assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1938-L2036)

**Footer and width algorithm.** Join tips with literal `" | "`. In order: `"tab to add notes"` (highlighted when selection exists and notes hidden), or `"tab or esc to clear notes"`; `"{key} to submit answer"` (highlighted for a single question) or highlighted `"{key} to submit all"` (last of multiple); then `"←/→ to navigate questions"` for options focus or `"ctrl + p / ctrl + n change question"` for freeform; finally `"{key} to interrupt"`. Omit the Esc interrupt tip while option notes are visible. Normal line is DIM; highlighted spans explicitly cyan+BOLD+**not DIM**. If options overflow vertically, prepend `"option {selected}/{total}"`. Greedily pack whole tips by display width; a too-wide tip gets its own line, then grapheme-safe word-boundary truncation reserving one column for `…`. Trim trailing whitespace; ellipsis inherits last span style. Width 0 returns empty, width 1 returns an unstyled ellipsis. [Tips/packing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L589-L696), [render](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L344-L380), [truncator](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L474-L572)

Options have no fixed eight-item cap. Their default two-column algorithm sets description column to `min(max_visible_name_width+2, width−1, max(floor(0.7×width),1))`; left width `max(desc_col−2,1)`, right width `max(width−desc_col,1)`. Wrap label independently with continuation indent equal to its numbered prefix width, clamped below left width; wrap description independently, DIM, and pad to the shared column. Selected visibility advances the item-window start until its wrapped row fits the viewport; the first row may be clipped if itself taller. Rendered options are bottom-aligned within their allocated block. [Columns/wrapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/selection_popup_common.rs#L136-L289), [viewport](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/selection_popup_common.rs#L347-L552), [bottom alignment](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L425-L466)

Desired height is at least 8: progress 1 + wrapped question + all measured options + notes composer (preferred height clamped 3–8) + footer + surface padding 2; add two spacers for options without notes, one with notes, zero for freeform. Actual layout with options preserves at least one option row, truncates question lines without ellipsis if needed, shrinks options to reserve progress/footer/spacers, then allocates notes. Without notes, remaining height grows options; with notes, remaining height grows notes. Freeform allocates question, notes, footer, progress in that priority order, then gives spare rows to notes. If its question alone overflows, only the truncated question remains. [Desired height](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/render.rs#L28-L107), [notes clamp](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1066-L1073), [exact allocation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/layout.rs#L64-L284)

### Question-result history: answered, skipped, and raw

Count an answer iff the matching map value exists and its `answers` vector is nonempty - even a vector containing an empty string counts. Preserve question order, ignore extra map keys. No option descriptions, question headers, IDs, timing, or success/error glyph is rendered. [Counting/rendering](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/request_user_input.rs#L13-L108)

| Segment               | Rich/transcript literal and style                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Header                | `"•"` DIM + `" "` default + `"Questions"` BOLD + `" {answered}/{total} answered"` DIM; optional `" (interrupted)"` cyan. Header itself is not prewrapped.                         |
| Question              | Initial `"  • "`, continuation `SP×4`, content default; append `" (unanswered)"` DIM to the **last already-wrapped line**, with no second width check.                            |
| Answer                | Initial `"    answer: "` DIM (12 columns), continuation `SP×12` DIM; content cyan. Separate answer entries repeat the label.                                                      |
| Note                  | Initial `"    note: "` DIM (10 columns), continuation `SP×10` DIM; content cyan. If `question.options` is `None`, use the answer label instead. `Some([])` still uses note label. |
| Secret answer         | One cyan `"••••••"` answer, regardless of entries or length; no note disclosure.                                                                                                  |
| Interrupted remainder | Only if interrupted and unanswered > 0: cyan+DIM `"  ↳ "` then cyan+DIM `"interrupted with {n} unanswered"`; continuation `SP×4` DIM.                                             |

Each body wraps through `adaptive_wrap_line` with total width `max(width,1)` and the stated prefixes. Entries beginning **exactly** `"user_note: "` are removed from the option list; the last such entry wins as the note. URL-like tokens retain adaptive wrapping's intact-token behavior, unlike the modal's plain question wrapping. [Rich renderer/helpers](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/request_user_input.rs#L28-L106), [note splitting](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/request_user_input.rs#L152-L186), [adaptive helper](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/wrapping.rs#L681-L761)

Raw emits `"Questions {answered}/{total} answered"`; optional separate `"(interrupted)"`; each question followed by `"answer: {option}"`, `"note: {note}"`, or separate `"(unanswered)"`. Each of those strings goes through `Line::from(String)`: embedded LF/CRLF separators disappear between adjacent spans on that one line. For example, note `"first\nsecond"` becomes visible raw `"note: firstsecond"`, not two lines. It has no bullets, indent, wrap, or final interrupted-remainder summary. Secrets become `"answer: ******"`. Freeform notes still use raw **note**, although rich uses **answer**. [Raw renderer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/request_user_input.rs#L110-L149)

Source-derived worked example, wide viewport: questions `"Choose a mode."` (options present) and `"Any constraint?"` (no options), only first answered `['Fast', 'user_note: Keep logs']`:

```text
• Questions 1/2 answered
  • Choose a mode.
    answer: Fast
    note: Keep logs
  • Any constraint? (unanswered)
```

Setting `interrupted=true` adds cyan `" (interrupted)"` to the header and `"  ↳ interrupted with 1 unanswered"` below. This demonstrates the renderer's capability, **not** a currently emitted interruption artifact. Styles come from the table/code, not this plain example. [Implementation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/request_user_input.rs#L28-L106), [non-emitting interruption branch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/request_user_input/mod.rs#L1238-L1244)

### Hook lifecycle and durable output

Hooks have their own active cell, independent of an exec cell. Begin flushes completed hook output, then inserts/resets a run by stable ID. End updates its matching run or creates an already-completed run when begin was absent. Persistent completed runs flush immediately, before following assistant output; other runs/timers remain active. [Lifecycle](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/hook_lifecycle.rs#L18-L88)

| State/event                                                        | Next state and visible result                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Begin, including duplicate ID                                      | `PendingReveal`, deadline now + **300 ms**; no rich/transcript/raw lines or activity summary. Duplicate resets status message and timer.    |
| Reveal timer due                                                   | `VisibleRunning { visible_since: now }`; activity summary only. The timestamp is actual reveal time, not original deadline.                 |
| Quiet success before reveal                                        | Remove immediately. Quiet = `status == Completed` and **all** entries are `Context`, including empty entries list.                          |
| Quiet success after reveal                                         | Remove if now ≥ visible_since + **600 ms**; otherwise `QuietLinger` until that deadline. Summary disappears immediately even during linger. |
| Completion with any non-Context entry, or any non-Completed status | `Completed { status, entries }`, persist immediately.                                                                                       |
| Replay/already-completed quiet success                             | Omit entirely. Other completions render normally.                                                                                           |
| Turn cancellation/completion without hook end                      | Drop active hook state, no synthetic failure/stop history. An explicit `Stopped` completion is a different event and does persist.          |

Sources: [constants/states](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L29-L77), [begin/end](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L172-L239), [timers](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L441-L519), [quiet predicate](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L504-L510), [turn cleanup](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/turn_runtime.rs#L320-L336).

Summary considers only `VisibleRunning`: trim messages, treat blank as absent; if all visible runs have the same nonempty message, use it; otherwise `"Running hook"` for one or `"Running hooks"` for several. No count/progress fraction, handler name, command, or duration. In the existing status row append DIM `" · " + message` only if the full addition fits; otherwise add a separate DIM `"  └ " + message` row with grapheme-safe `…` truncation. Without a status row, render DIM `"• "` plus first-character-capitalized shimmering text, whole line DIM, one row with ellipsis. A modal takes precedence over these activity rows. [Summary](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L132-L157), [inline/overflow](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/status_indicator_widget.rs#L252-L274), [standalone](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/hook_status.rs#L18-L32), [modal priority](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/mod.rs#L1973-L2000), [ellipsis](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/line_truncation.rs#L76-L101)

Standalone shimmer uses a process-synchronized 2-second sweep, 10-character padding, half-band width 5; intensity is `(1+cos(PI*(distance/5)))/2` inside the band, zero outside. Timing, constants, intermediates, and blending use the shared **f32** arithmetic and source parenthesization, not ordinary JavaScript doubles. Truecolor spans are BOLD RGB blended from default foreground (fallback 128,128,128) toward default background (fallback white), alpha `0.9×intensity`; no span background. Nontruecolor uses DIM below .2, default below .6, BOLD otherwise; parent line DIM remains. Reduced motion emits plain text under parent DIM. Hook timers still run without animations; animated visible/lingering hooks additionally request 50-ms frames. [Shimmer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/shimmer.rs#L21-L80), [reduced motion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/motion.rs#L52-L63), [scheduling](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/hook_lifecycle.rs#L131-L151)

Durable rendering uses **only the first Warning** as a system message. For successful status plus Warning, header is DIM `"↳ Hook · "` + first warning line default. Otherwise use the following bullet/header pair; space and header text are default, never BOLD:

| Status                                   | Bullet           | Header              |
| ---------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------- |
| Completed                                | green+BOLD `"•"` | `"Hook completed"`  |
| Failed                                   | red+BOLD `"•"`   | `"Hook failed"`     |
| Blocked                                  | red+BOLD `"•"`   | `"Blocked by hook"` |
| Stopped                                  | red+BOLD `"•"`   | `"Hook stopped"`    |
| Running supplied as a completion summary | default `"•"`    | `"Hook running"`    |

[Header construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L280-L307), [bullet styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L512-L518).

When the warning is not used in the successful-status header, its first line is `"  └ {text}"`; later nonempty lines get `SP×4`, empty lines remain empty. Then emit every non-Warning/non-Context entry in original order with the same first/continuation prefixes. Thus later Warnings are discarded; Context is always hidden; Error/Feedback/Stop bodies are **default**, not red. Separate runs by one empty line. Width is ignored by the cell: no entry limit, line limit, truncation, syntax highlighting, or special transcript expansion. Raw simply removes styles while retaining glyphs/indent/blank lines. [Renderer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L271-L339)

Worked examples established by committed assertions: successful Warning `"Heads up from the hook"` plus private Context yields exactly `"↳ Hook · Heads up from the hook"` on rich/transcript/raw, with only provenance DIM in rich; failed Error `"Policy prevented this action."` yields `"• Hook failed\n  └ Policy prevented this action."`, red+BOLD bullet and default body. Five-line non-Context outputs remain complete even at width 20. [Style assertions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L530-L571), [untruncated output and failure styles](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/hook_cell.rs#L623-L678)

The separate persisted-thread transcript loader has a distinct **HookPrompt** fallback: one line per fragment, DIM `"hook prompt: "` plus default `fragment.text.trim()`, stored as PlainHistoryCell. Main ChatWidget replay ignores that item. This is not a HookCell `Context` entry; the Context-hiding guarantee above must not be generalized to every persisted hook-related item. [Main replay](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/replay.rs#L235-L258), [persisted fallback](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/thread_transcript.rs#L187-L209), [cell construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/thread_transcript.rs#L303-L312)

### Approval modal: exact choices and preview

Exec decision order comes from `available_decisions` unchanged except filtered multiline exec-policy prefixes. If absent: network context defaults to Accept, AcceptForSession, first proposed Allow rule if any, Cancel; additional permissions defaults to Accept, Cancel; ordinary command defaults to Accept, optional prefix amendment, Cancel. Explicit empty decisions remain empty. Approval ID prefers `approval_id`, otherwise `call_id`. [Defaults](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/approval_events.rs#L49-L111)

Titles are BOLD, followed by an empty line and body:

| Request                                 | Title                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Exec                                    | `"Would you like to run the following command?"`                                                                                        |
| Network exec                            | `"Do you want to approve network access to \"{host}\"?"`                                                                                |
| WriteStdin                              | `"Would you like to send input to terminal {command[2]}?"`; missing index 2: `"Would you like to send input to the existing terminal?"` |
| Permissions                             | `"Would you like to grant these permissions?"`                                                                                          |
| Patch                                   | `"Would you like to make the following edits?"`                                                                                         |
| Basic MCP elicitation approval fallback | `"{server_name} needs your approval."`                                                                                                  |

Exec/permissions body fields, when present, are `"Thread: "` + BOLD label, `"Environment: "` + BOLD ID, `"Reason: "` + ITALIC reason; each followed by an empty line. `"Permission rule: "` has cyan value. Exec then displays highlighted command with default `"$ "` inserted on its **first** line only, except network context suppresses command entirely. WriteStdin instead displays `"Input: "` + Rust debug-quoted last argument (missing → `"\"\""`). These bodies wrap with `trim=false`.
