[Titles/options assembly](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L247-L322), [body](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L690-L784)

Command normalization extracts a recognized shell script; otherwise `shlex::try_join`, falling back to space joining. Highlighting uses Bash regardless of originating shell: theme-derived foreground and BOLD only, no token backgrounds, italic, or underline. Plain fallback applies for unavailable syntax/highlight errors or >512 KiB total, >10,000 lines, or any line >4 KiB. [Normalization](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_command.rs#L8-L17), [shell extraction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/shell-command/src/parse_command.rs#L39-L42), [style conversion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L492-L533), [limits/fallback](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/render/highlight.rs#L584-L702)

Permission rule parts appear in order `"network"`, `"read {paths}"`, `"write {paths}"`, `"deny read {paths}"`, joined `"; "`; network requires `enabled=true`. Paths join `", "`; ordinary/special paths are backtick-quoted, glob is ``"glob `{pattern}`"``. Special labels: `:root`, `:minimal`, `:workspace_roots[/subpath]`, `:tmpdir`, `/tmp`, or unknown base plus optional `/subpath`. Empty rule omitted. Patch body instead uses `"Description: "` + ITALIC nonempty reason or `"Apply proposed file edits"`, then `"Destination: "` + BOLD path for each sorted/deduplicated source and move destination. Infer native path from URI where possible, otherwise join cwd; no paths gives BOLD `"unavailable"`. This modal contains destinations, **not the patch diff**. [Permissions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L917-L1010), [patch body](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/apply_patch_header.rs#L12-L74)

| Decision                                        | Literal label; default shortcut                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Exec Accept                                     | `"Yes, proceed"`; network: `"Yes, just this once"`; `y`                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Exec AcceptForSession                           | Network: `"Yes, and allow this host for this conversation"`; additional permissions: `"Yes, and allow these permissions for this session"`; otherwise `"Yes, and don't ask again for this command in this session"`; `a`                                                     |
| Exec prefix amendment                           | ``"Yes, and don't ask again for commands that start with `{prefix}`"``; `p`; omit option if normalized prefix contains CR or LF.                                                                                                                                             |
| Network rule Allow / Deny                       | `"Yes, and allow this host in the future"` (`p`) / `"No, and block this host in the future"` (`d`)                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Exec Decline                                    | `"No, continue without running it"`; `d`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Exec Cancel / patch Cancel                      | `"No, and tell Codex what to do differently"`; `esc`, `n`                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Patch Accept / AcceptForSession                 | `"Yes, proceed"` (`y`) / `"Yes, and don't ask again for these files"` (`a`)                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Permissions turn / strict turn / session / deny | `"Yes, grant these permissions for this turn"` (`y`); `"Yes, grant for this turn with strict auto review"` (hardcoded `r`); `"Yes, grant these permissions for this session"` (`a`); `"No, continue without permissions"` (`d`, with plain Esc filtered from deny bindings). |
| Basic elicitation Accept / Decline / Cancel     | `"Yes, provide the requested info"` (`y`); `"No, but continue without it"` (`n`); `"Cancel this request"` (`esc`, `c`). Cancel always includes Esc; overlaps are removed from Decline.                                                                                       |

Sources: [exec choices](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L829-L915), [other choices](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L1012-L1106), [default bindings](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/keymap.rs#L1727-L1775).

Rows use `"› {n}. "` selected / `"  {n}. "` unselected, followed by label and `" ({shortcut})"` when a hint exists. Shortcut span DIM on unselected rows; selected spans all become accent+BOLD. First enabled item initially selected. No search field, descriptions, disabled choices, or mouse handler is added by this overlay. Enter confirms; digit directly activates; list navigation supports default arrows/`j`/`k`/Ctrl+N/P/J/K, PageUp/Down or Ctrl+B/F, Home/End. Approval shortcuts run before list handling; configured list Cancel precedes decision shortcuts. Ctrl+C cancels. [Row construction](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/list_selection_view.rs#L566-L638), [shortcut spans](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/selection_row_layout.rs#L120-L164), [input routing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L541-L598), [list input](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/list_selection_view.rs#L982-L1087)

Approval layout differs from questions: header width `W−4`; numbered rows start **two columns left of the inset**, with render width `max(W−2,1)` and continuation indent equal to numbered-prefix width. Height measurement uses `W−1`, up to **8 items**, and adds surface padding 2, header gap 1, footer 1. Labels word-wrap rather than being command-snippet-truncated. If the header loses vertical space, reserve its last row for DIM `"[… {header_height} lines] ctrl + a view all"`; that text hardcodes the shortcut. The one-line footer lies **outside** the colored surface, inset left 2, all DIM: normally `"Press enter to confirm or esc to cancel"`; remapped/unbound hints substitute/omit their clauses. Cross-thread prompts append `" or {key} to open thread"` (default `o`). Footer clips rather than wraps. [Layout/measurement](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/list_selection_view.rs#L1147-L1294), [row placement](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/list_selection_view.rs#L1315-L1348), [footer](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/list_selection_view.rs#L1431-L1440), [footer composition](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L635-L659)

Fullscreen preview (default Ctrl+A or Ctrl+Shift+A) emits `FullScreenApprovalRequest` without deciding. Pager titles: `"P A T C H"` for full DiffSummary; `"E X E C"` for full highlighted normalized command **without `$ `**; `"P E R M I S S I O N S"` for environment/reason/rule; `"E L I C I T A T I O N"` for server/message. It is a preview, not a raw/transcript form of the pending modal; no approval buttons/history are created there. [Dispatch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/app/event_dispatch.rs#L2906-L2968)

### Decision history: command, network, patch, and permissions

Command decisions insert history only when `thread_label` is absent; cross-thread decisions still route to that thread but do not add a local notice. `Accept/AcceptForSession/Decline/Cancel` map to `Approved/ApprovedForSession/Denied/Abort`. Network target prefers synthetic command `['network-access', target]` or single string `"network-access {target}"`; otherwise construct protocol `http`, `https`, `socks5-tcp`, or `socks5-udp` plus `"://{host}"`. [Decision routing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L367-L404), [target](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L661-L688), [mapping](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L808-L827)

The following templates exclude their prefix. `A` is exactly `"You "` or `"Auto-reviewer "` (trailing space included); `C` is DIM command snippet, `T` DIM target. **Bold fragments** below are the exact BOLD spans; all other text is default. Prefix is green `"✔ "` for approvals/Allow, red `"✗ "` otherwise, neither bold. Continuation prefix is `SP×2`, default. [Templates](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/approvals.rs#L45-L277)

| Decision         | Command summary                                                                                     | Network summary                                                         |
| ---------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Approved         | A + **approved** + `" codex to run "` + C + **` this time`**                                        | A + **approved** + `" codex network access to "` + T + **` this time`** |
| Session          | Same, suffix **` every time this session`**                                                         | Same, suffix **` every time this session`**                             |
| Prefix amendment | A + **approved** + `" codex to always run commands that start with "` + DIM amended-command snippet | Same implementation, independent of subject                             |
| Persist Allow    | A + **persisted** + `" Codex network access to "` + DIM amendment host                              | Same, but uses T; capital `Codex` is intentional                        |
| Persist Deny     | A + **denied** + `" codex network access to "` + DIM amendment host + `" and saved that rule"`      | Same, but uses T                                                        |
| Denied, user     | A + **did not approve** + `" codex to run "` + C                                                    | A + **did not approve** + `" codex network access to "` + T             |
| Denied, guardian | `"Request "` + **denied** + `" for codex to run "` + C                                              | Uses the ordinary A + **did not approve** network template              |
| TimedOut         | `"Review "` + **timed out** + `" before codex could run "` + C                                      | `"Review "` + **timed out** + `" before codex could access "` + T       |
| Abort            | A + **canceled** + `" the request to run "` + C                                                     | A + **canceled** + `" the request for codex network access to "` + T    |

Empty command snippet substitutes: Approved/session → A + **approved** + `" this request"` + normal bold time suffix; user Denied → A + **did not approve** + `" this request"`; guardian Denied → `"Request "` + **denied**; TimedOut → `"Review "` + **timed out** + `" before this request could be approved"`; Abort → A + **canceled** + `" this request"`. Prefix amendments do not use this empty-command fallback. [Branches](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/approvals.rs#L54-L251)

Snippet algorithm: normalize command; if LF exists, keep first line and append ASCII `" ..."`; then truncate to **80 extended graphemes**, replacing overflow with the first **77** graphemes plus ASCII `"..."`. No word-boundary cut; CR alone is not the multiline delimiter. Adaptive wrapping applies after truncation; width 0 returns no rich lines. Raw discards the symbol/indent and styles, but **retains the already-truncated snippet**; transcript is identical to rich. [Snippet](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/approvals.rs#L5-L22), [grapheme truncation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/text_formatting.rs#L88-L114), [wrapped/raw cell](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/base.rs#L61-L95)

Example from the committed multiline test input `['bash','-lc','echo line1\necho line2']`: pressing `n` yields rich `"✗ You canceled the request to run echo line1 ..."`; raw omits `"✗ "`. Only `canceled` is BOLD, snippet DIM, prefix red. For `"echo " + 'a'×200`, snippet is `"echo " + 'a'×72 + "..."`. [Test input/decision](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/approval_requests.rs#L418-L503), [style branch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/approvals.rs#L230-L265)

**Patch and permission exceptions.** Ordinary user patch acceptance/session acceptance/cancellation sends `patch_approval` but inserts **no decision history notice** here. Guardian denial uses red `"✗ "` + `"Request "` + BOLD `"denied"` + `" for codex to apply a patch touching "` + DIM single path, or DIM count + `" files"`. Guardian timeout substitutes `"Review "` + BOLD `"timed out"` + `" before codex could apply a patch touching "`. Zero files uses `"0 files"`. Generic guardian action notices use `"Request "` + BOLD `"denied"` + `" for "` + DIM summary, or `"Review "` + BOLD `"timed out"` + `" before "` + DIM summary. Pending guardian review owns live status rather than history; **Approved guardian assessments silently finish**. Denied/timed-out requests create the notices above. [Patch routing](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L456-L466), [guardian cells](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/approvals.rs#L280-L343), [guardian lifecycle](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_requests.rs#L98-L202)

Permissions decisions are **unstyled PlainHistoryCell** messages, without bullets, wrapping logic, or a list of granted paths: `"You did not grant additional permissions"` if the resulting profile is empty; else strict → `"You granted additional permissions with strict auto review"`; else session → `"You granted additional permissions for this session"`; otherwise `"You granted additional permissions"`. Denial returns empty permissions, turn scope, strict=false; session grants use session scope; strict grants use turn scope and strict=true. Cross-thread label suppresses local notice. Raw equals rich text with styles removed. Basic elicitation decisions similarly route a response but add no notice in this overlay. [Permissions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L406-L454), [elicitation](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/bottom_pane/approval_overlay.rs#L468-L489), [plain projection](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/base.rs#L5-L24)

## Pi integration

### Evidence and integration boundary

This chapter distinguishes **observed Pi/workspace behavior** from **recommendations**. Installed evidence is `@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent` **0.85.1**, rooted at `/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent`; citations below identify shipped JavaScript/declarations, not an assumed upstream TypeScript revision. The workspace package is `pi-tool-display` **0.1.0**, exporting its default extension and `pi-tool-display/renderers`. [Installed manifest:1–18](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/package.json#L1-L18), [workspace manifest:1–23](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/package.json#L1-L23).

The existing package explicitly owns **individual tool-call rows**. Cross-call grouping, transcript pagers, user `!` shell cards, and HTML exports are outside its declared scope. It prohibits separate image-byte retention, accumulated streaming deltas, and timers. These are current package boundaries, not missing Pi APIs to implement inside this package. [Workspace constraints:9–13](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/AGENTS.md#L9-L13).

### Registration: a tool override, not a public renderer registry

| Seam                            | Observed contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `pi.registerTool(definition)`   | Requires the complete definition, including `name`, `label`, `description`, `parameters`, and `execute`; optional renderer slots belong to that definition. Registration stores by name and refreshes the runtime.                                                                       |
| Renderer-only tool registration | **Absent from the installed `ExtensionAPI`.** `registerMessageRenderer`, `registerEntryRenderer`, and `registerMarkdownTransformer` target other content; none decorates arbitrary existing tool names. Internal renderer-only modules exist, but are not an extension registration API. |
| Same-name replacement           | Replaces a built-in execution definition. Within one extension, another registration replaces its map entry; across extensions, the first extension in load order wins. SDK `customTools` are applied after extension registrations in the session registry.                             |
| Missing renderer slots          | The TUI lookup independently fills missing `renderCall` and `renderResult` from built-ins using `??`. `renderShell` remains the supplied value; missing means `"default"`. Missing prompt metadata is not inherited by the execution registry.                                           |
| Definition access               | `pi.getAllTools()` returns metadata, **not** `execute`, renderer functions, `label`, or `promptSnippet`. `AgentSession.getToolDefinition(name)` exists on the session, but is not exposed through `ExtensionAPI`.                                                                        |

Evidence: [definition types:342–377](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/extensions/types.d.ts#L342-L377), [registration surface:939–970](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/extensions/types.d.ts#L939-L970), [loader:238–285](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/extensions/loader.js#L238-L285), [extension precedence:324–335](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/extensions/runner.js#L324-L335), [session registry:2105–2158](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/agent-session.js#L2105-L2158), [slot inheritance:35–45](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/tools/renderers/index.js#L35-L45), [metadata lookup:641–652](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/agent-session.js#L641-L652).

**Current workspace:** at TUI `session_start`, read trusted execution settings; construct Pi-owned built-in definitions; spread `createToolDisplayRenderers(name)` over them; restore the active tool list. The guard skips names already owned by extensions/SDK custom tools. It reconstructs ordinary CLI execution rather than retrieving the original closure. SDK base-tool overrides also receive `source: "builtin"`, so the default extension cannot safely identify them. SDK/MCP owners must decorate their own original definitions. Already-overridden tools are protected; arbitrary later dynamic registrations still follow Pi's first-extension precedence. [Workspace entry:6–33](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/index.ts#L6-L33), [registration:26–69](#historical-workspace-sources), [SDK base definitions:2182–2201](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/agent-session.js#L2182-L2201).

The existing opt-in interface is sufficient for owner-controlled tools:

```ts
import { createToolDisplayRenderers } from "pi-tool-display/renderers";

pi.registerTool({
  ...originalDefinition,
  ...createToolDisplayRenderers(originalDefinition.name, "invocation")
});
```

This preserves execution/schema/metadata by construction; it does not decorate another extension's tool after registration. [Workspace integration contract:3–16](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/docs/renderers.md#L3-L16).

### Renderer context and lifecycle truth table

Signatures are `renderCall(args, theme, context): Component` and `renderResult(result, { expanded, isPartial }, theme, context): Component`. Context contains `args`, `toolCallId`, `cwd`, `state`, `lastComponent`, `invalidate`, `executionStarted`, `argsComplete`, `isPartial`, `expanded`, `showImages`, and `isError`. There is **no** status enum, abort reason, elapsed time, exit code, replay flag, viewport height, or sibling ownership reference. In the TUI, `result` passed to the callback contains only `content` and `details`; read failure from `context.isError`, not `result.isError`. [Types:307–377](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/extensions/types.d.ts#L307-L377), [host context:72–89](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L72-L89), [result invocation:251–273](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L251-L273).

`state` starts as a fresh `{}` per host row and is shared by both slots. Each slot has its own `lastComponent`; it is not the previous result payload. The host runs call rendering before result rendering on each display rebuild. A result renderer is not invoked until some result exists, including an empty-content partial update. Callback exceptions clear that slot's saved component and use its fallback; this catch does not surround a component's later `render(width)` call. [Host storage/construction:7–58](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L7-L58), [composition:216–277](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L216-L277).

Here `E = executionStarted`, `A = argsComplete`, `P = isPartial`, `X = isError`:

| Host event/state                                          |         E |         A |   P |           X | Result slot                                        |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | --------: | --------: | --: | ----------: | -------------------------------------------------- |
| New streamed call                                         |         0 |         0 |   1 |           0 | Not called                                         |
| Normal assistant message finishes                         |         0 |         1 |   1 |           0 | Not called without update                          |
| Execution-start on existing row                           |         1 | unchanged |   1 |           0 | Unchanged                                          |
| Execution-start creates missing row                       |         1 |     **0** |   1 |           0 | Not called                                         |
| Partial execution result                                  | unchanged | unchanged |   1 |       **0** | Latest partial payload                             |
| Final success/error                                       | unchanged | unchanged |   0 |  event flag | Final payload replaces partial                     |
| Assistant abort/error before execution                    | usually 0 | unchanged |   0 |           1 | Synthetic error text                               |
| Replayed normal assistant, stored final result            |     **0** |     **0** |   0 | stored flag | Stored payload                                     |
| Replayed normal assistant, no matching result             |         0 |         0 |   1 |           0 | Not called                                         |
| Replayed aborted assistant, with or without stored result |         0 |         0 |   0 |           1 | Synthetic abort text                               |
| Replayed error assistant, with or without stored result   |         0 |         0 |   0 |           1 | Truthy `message.errorMessage`, otherwise `"Error"` |

Here a normal assistant has neither `stopReason: "aborted"` nor `"error"`. The exceptional replay branches synthesize a final result immediately and do not register that row for stored-result matching; a later matching result does not replace the synthetic error. Abort wording uses the **current session's** `retryAttempt`, not a recovered historical retry count: zero → `"Operation aborted"`; positive → `"Aborted after N retry attempt"`, plural `"attempts"` when `N>1`. [Replay exception and literals:3025–3067](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L3025-L3067).

These flags are assignments, not a complete execution-state machine. In particular, **`E=1` includes validation/permission preflight**, and `E=0` does not imply pending after replay. `context.args` is the host's stored argument object; execution-start does not replace an existing row's arguments with a separately validated/prepared object. [Flag setters:115–134](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L115-L134), [live handlers:2622–2724](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L2622-L2724), [replay:3012–3067](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L3012-L3067).

`context.invalidate()` synchronously invalidates/rebuilds the row, then requests a TUI render. Component `invalidate()` only clears/rebuilds that component's caches. Consequently, do not call `context.invalidate()` unconditionally inside renderer callbacks. Cache width-dependent rows on the returned component and regenerate theme-dependent strings after invalidation. The workspace already reuses `lastComponent` this way and never accumulates partial results. Its context type is extracted from `ToolDefinition`, because `ToolRenderContext` is absent from Pi's package-root exports. [Context invalidation:71–89](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L71-L89), [component invalidation:172–175](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L172-L175), [workspace component/type:27–87](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L27-L87), [root type exports:7](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/index.d.ts#L7-L7).

### Event ordering, aborts, and replay

**Observed execution order:** assistant message finalization → `tool_execution_start` → argument preparation/validation → `tool_call` → execution/update events → `tool_result` → `tool_execution_end` → final tool-result message events. Unknown tools, validation failures, blocked calls, and pre-execution aborts take an immediate-result branch and **skip `tool_result`**. A thrown execution error becomes text plus `details: {}`, with `isError=true`; returning a result normally yields `isError=false` before result middleware. Late `onUpdate` calls are ignored after execution settles; already-emitted update promises drain before finalization. [Agent loop:294–552](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-agent-core/dist/agent-loop.js#L294-L552).

Parallel execution preflights in assistant source order, then launches prepared calls together. Updates and finalization interleave; final result messages are emitted in original call order after `Promise.all`. Extension event handlers run before UI subscribers; `message_end` persistence occurs **after** both. A renderer or `message_end` observer cannot assume the current message already exists in `sessionManager`. [Parallel loop:330–382](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-agent-core/dist/agent-loop.js#L330-L382), [session dispatch:360–428](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/agent-session.js#L360-L428).

Abort has no distinct renderer flag. Assistant abort synthesizes `"Operation aborted"`, or `"Aborted after N retry attempt"` / `"Aborted after N retry attempts"`; assistant error uses its error message or `"Error"`. Bash execution abort instead throws retained output followed by `"Command aborted"`; timeout appends `"Command timed out after N seconds"`; nonzero exit appends `"Command exited with code N"`. When output exists, each status is separated from it by exactly `"\n\n"`. These all reach the workspace's error rendering, not an independent cancelled state. [Assistant terminal handling:2651–2683](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L2651-L2683), [bash terminal outcomes:237–264](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/tools/bash.js#L237-L264).

At `agent_end`, Pi clears pending-row tracking without synthesizing another result, so an unfinished row is not automatically marked successful. [Agent-end cleanup:2726–2739](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L2726-L2739).

Replay directly creates rows from assistant tool calls and, except for the assistant abort/error branches above, matches stored results by ID; it does not re-emit execution events. Unmatched results create no row. Rebuild uses compaction-aware `buildContextEntries()`, resets row state, and supplies the current session cwd, not a separately persisted per-call cwd. Startup binds extensions before initial rendering; `/reload` deliberately rebuilds chat **before** the new `session_start`. Existing rows retain their constructor-supplied definitions. [Replay:2991–3087](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L2991-L3087), [rebuild:3193–3196](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L3193-L3196), [startup:711–720](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L711-L720), [reload:2217–2240](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/agent-session.js#L2217-L2240).

**Current workspace workaround:** install a render-only wrapper around private `InteractiveMode.prototype.getRegisteredToolDefinition` during factory loading. It decorates only current-session built-in ownership, uses `Symbol.for("pi-tool-display.replayHook")` to replace earlier installations, and becomes inert on disposal without removing a later extension's wrapper. No execution definition is changed by this hook. Missing private lookup produces a warning. [Replay implementation:14–51](#historical-workspace-sources), [warning:16–20](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/index.ts#L16-L20).

### Host spacing, images, and expansion

`renderShell: "self"` removes the default `Box(1, 1)` padding/background. It does **not** remove host row ownership:

- Nonempty self-rendered content receives exactly one leading `""` row, no host horizontal padding, and no host-inserted separator between call/result slots.
- Empty call and result components can suppress the entire text row and leading spacer. Host images still render independently, each with its own `Spacer(1)`.
- Default shells use pending/error/success background by `P ? pending : X ? error : success`. Undefined definitions use generic name/JSON/output rendering without that preview limit. Defined tools with a missing/throwing result callback use a **10-logical-line head preview**, not a ten-screen-row budget. The call fallback is bold `toolTitle(name)`; result lines use `toolOutput`, followed by literal `"... (N more lines, KEY to expand)"` when shortened (`muted` punctuation/count/description, `dim` key). Empty fallback output adds no result component.

[Shell construction:43–57](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L43-L57), [self projection:176–204](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L176-L204), [background/composition:216–277](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L216-L277), [fallback:90–106](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L90-L106), [generic fallback:312–327](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L312-L327).

Images are appended outside both renderer slots when capability detection, `showImages`, `data`, and `mimeType` permit. Kitty requires PNG; other formats convert asynchronously and trigger another display rebuild. Default preferred width is **60 cells**; `Image.render(w)` uses `max(1,min(w−2,preferred))`, and default maximum height is `max(1,ceil(maxWidth × cellWidthPx / cellHeightPx))`. Expansion does not gate images. Custom result renderers must handle textual fallback themselves: the generic helper only adds image indicators when images are unsupported/hidden. The workspace instead always inserts `"[Image: MIME]"` into normalized result text, so non-read previews can show that marker **and** the host image. [Host image conversion:136–158](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L136-L158), [image append:282–311](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L282-L311), [image sizing:28–46](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-tui/dist/components/image.js#L28-L46), [fallback helper:35–53](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/tools/render-utils.js#L35-L53), [workspace media text:66–74](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/presentation.ts#L66-L74).

`app.tools.expand` defaults to **Ctrl+O**; `app.interrupt` to **Escape**; **Ctrl+T toggles thinking**, not a Codex-style transcript pager. Global expansion also updates other expandable host children and new rows. Fullscreen left-click on a call/result region toggles that one row after any result exists, including a partial result; it does not change the global setting. [Keybindings:125–169](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/docs/keybindings.md#L125-L169), [global expansion:3460–3479](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L3460-L3479), [row click:106–112](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/tool-execution.js#L106-L112).

Use configured hints: `keyText("app.tools.expand")` joins alternatives with `"/"`, returns `""` for an unbound action, and displays `alt` as `option` on macOS. `keyHint` styles key text `dim` and the one-space-prefixed description `muted`; the workspace instead applies one surrounding style to its entire hint. [Hint helper:6–31](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/keybinding-hints.js#L6-L31).

### Current workspace projection: literals, styles, and budgets

Quoted prefixes below are exact strings; each ordinary space is U+0020. No explicit background is applied by the workspace. Foreground “default” means terminal default, **not** Pi's `text` token.

| Span/behavior                    | Exact current implementation                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Heading                          | `glyph + " " + bold(phase) + " " + accent(detail)`                                                                                                                                  |
| Glyph                            | Pending `"◦"` U+25E6; settled `"•"` U+2022. Error foreground `error`; otherwise pending `warning`; successful read/search/list `dim`; other success `success`.                      |
| Phase                            | Pending before execution: `"Preparing"` when args incomplete, otherwise `"Queued"`. Else error: `"Failed " + name`; otherwise family verb below. **Bold only**, foreground default. |
| Family verbs                     | `Reading/Read`, `Searching/Searched`, `Listing/Listed`, `Running/Ran`, `Editing/Edited`, `Writing/Wrote`, `Calling/Called`.                                                         |
| Body gutter                      | First `"  └ "`; continuation `"    "`; default foreground, no modifiers. Header continuation uses `"  │ "`.                                                                         |
| Ordinary output                  | `muted`; error output `error`; no modifiers. Empty partial `"Waiting for output…"`; empty final `"No output"`.                                                                      |
| Successful collapsed read        | `dim`: `"N lines returned (KEY to expand)"`, `"N image(s) (KEY to expand)"`, or `"Empty file (KEY to expand)"`. Count normalized returned text, not source-file metadata.           |
| Edit success with `details.diff` | `muted` summary `"+A -R"`, then Pi `renderDiff(diff)` using a separate budget. No submitted-edit preview before a result.                                                           |
| Expanded write                   | Submitted `args.content` is highlighted in the **call slot**; result first prefix becomes `"    "` when content is nonempty. This is not a reread of the resulting file.            |

[Family verbs:43–51](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L43-L51), [heading spans:89–132](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L89-L132), [result projection:152–231](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L152-L231), [gutters:44–57](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/layout.ts#L44-L57), [diff counts:94–103](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/presentation.ts#L94-L103).

Arguments use `path ?? file_path`; non-string paths become empty. A read range appears only when `offset` or `limit` is supplied: `":" + offset + optional("-" + (offset+limit−1))`; nonnumeric/missing offset defaults to 1, and only numeric limits produce the end suffix. Search detail is `"PATTERN in PATH (GLOB)"`, with pattern fallback `"…"`, path fallback `"."`, and optional glob suffix. Shell detail is command or `"…"`, plus `" (timeout Ns)"` for a numeric timeout. Invocation detail is `name + "(" + JSON.stringify(argsObject) + ")"`. This is name/family interpretation, **not shell-command parsing into Codex exploration operations**. [Argument interpretation:7–65](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/presentation.ts#L7-L65).

Width algorithm:

1. Normalize tool text by stripping terminal sequences, mapping CRLF/bare CR to LF, tabs to **three spaces**, and removing remaining C0/C1 controls. Remove trailing LFs from combined result text.
2. Body width `b=max(1,w−4)`. ANSI-aware word-wrap, clamp each row to `b`, then prefix and clamp to `w`. A wide grapheme that cannot fit is discarded; widths at/below the gutter may retain no body text.
3. Collapsed ordinary body budget **5 visual rows**, diff budget **12**. For `n>m`, retain `ceil((m−1)/2)` head and `floor((m−1)/2)` tail rows; insert `"… K more rows (KEY to expand)"`, where `K=n−(m−1)`. Expanded retains all available rows.
4. Heading: if `w−visibleWidth(title)−1 >= 8`, wrap the entire heading at `max(1,w−4)` and prefix continuation rows. Otherwise put the clipped title on its own row and wrap detail at `b`. Collapsed headings retain three rows **plus** a fourth `"  │ … (KEY to expand)"` hint.
5. Highlighting is skipped above **512×1024 UTF-16 code units**, **10,000 lines**, or any line above **4096 code units**. Skipping does not truncate retained text.

[Normalization/layout:8–57](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/layout.ts#L8-L57), [result trimming:66–74](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/presentation.ts#L66-L74), [heading/highlight:105–144](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L105-L144).

Backend notices append **outside** the compact budget, foreground `warning`, prefix `"    "`. Exact supported notices are `"Tool output truncated by pi; expand to inspect retained output."`, `"Saved output: PATH"`, `"Match limit reached."`, `"Result limit reached."`, `"Entry limit reached."`, and `"Long result lines truncated by pi."`; their triggers are respectively `truncation.truncated===true`, string `fullOutputPath`, numeric limit fields, and `linesTruncated===true`. [Notice algorithm:77–94](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/presentation.ts#L77-L94).

**Retention limitation:** Pi's backend defaults are **2000 lines / 50×1024 bytes**. Bash updates contain bounded snapshots, not deltas; the extension replaces them. Bash emits an initial empty result and throttles subsequent updates at **100 ms**, with a final flush. [Native update constant:14–15](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/tools/renderers/bash.js#L14-L15). Final bash errors lose structured truncation details when converted from thrown errors to `{details:{}}`, although the error text can retain a saved-output notice. That text-only notice is subject to the normal compact budget; the workspace cannot guarantee separately pinned omission notices without recognized metadata. Expansion never recovers discarded bytes. [Limits:10–12](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/tools/truncate.js#L10-L12), [bash snapshots/outcomes:162–264](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/tools/bash.js#L162-L264), [error conversion:479–489](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-agent-core/dist/agent-loop.js#L479-L489), [error shape:526–531](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-agent-core/dist/agent-loop.js#L526-L531).

Worked examples, derived from those algorithms at width 80 with default keys:

```text
[host blank row]
• Read src/a.ts:10-11
  └ 2 lines returned (ctrl+o to expand)
```

For replayed `read({path:"src/a.ts",offset:10,limit:2})` returning `"alpha\nbeta\n"`, both execution/argument flags remain false, but `P=false` selects `Read`. Bullet and summary use `dim`; `Read` is bold/default foreground; detail uses `accent`; gutters remain default. Expanding displays the retained text instead. [Replay state:3016–3057](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L3016-L3057), [workspace read branch:192–205](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L192-L205).

For eight short shell-output lines numbered `0` through `7`, the five-row body is exactly `"  └ 0"`, `"    1"`, `"    … 4 more rows (ctrl+o to expand)"`, `"    6"`, `"    7"`. The omission row is `muted` like the output, not `dim`; its gutter is default. [Budget:29–41](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/layout.ts#L29-L41), [body styling:152–172](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L152-L172).

### Theme mapping and fidelity limits

These are **resolved built-in Pi values**, not Codex colors. Tokens apply foreground only unless listed as backgrounds; they do not imply bold/dim/italic modifiers. [Dark palette:4–74](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/theme/dark.json#L4-L74), [light palette:4–73](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/theme/light.json#L4-L73).

| Token(s)                                  | Dark      | Light     |
| ----------------------------------------- | --------- | --------- |
| `text`, `toolTitle`                       | `#d4d4d4` | `#1f2328` |
| `accent`                                  | `#8abeb7` | `#5a8080` |
| `muted`, `toolOutput`, `toolDiffContext`  | `#808080` | `#6c6c6c` |
| `dim`                                     | `#666666` | `#767676` |
| `success`, `toolDiffAdded`, `mdCodeBlock` | `#b5bd68` | `#588458` |
| `error`, `toolDiffRemoved`                | `#cc6666` | `#aa5555` |
| `warning`                                 | `#ffff00` | `#9a7326` |
| `toolPendingBg`                           | `#282832` | `#e8e8f0` |
| `toolSuccessBg`                           | `#283228` | `#e8f0e8` |
| `toolErrorBg`                             | `#3c2828` | `#f0e8e8` |
| `syntaxComment`                           | `#6A9955` | `#008000` |
| `syntaxKeyword`                           | `#569CD6` | `#0000FF` |
| `syntaxFunction`                          | `#DCDCAA` | `#795E26` |
| `syntaxVariable`                          | `#9CDCFE` | `#001080` |
| `syntaxString`                            | `#CE9178` | `#A31515` |
| `syntaxNumber`                            | `#B5CEA8` | `#098658` |
| `syntaxType`                              | `#4EC9B0` | `#267F99` |
| `syntaxOperator`, `syntaxPunctuation`     | `#D4D4D4` | `#000000` |

`theme.fg` ends with SGR **39**, `theme.bg` with **49**; neither restores an enclosing foreground/background. An empty theme value emits terminal-default color, whereas built-in `text` is explicit RGB. `bold`, `italic`, `underline`, `inverse`, and `strikethrough` delegate to Chalk. There is no `theme.dim()` method: `fg("dim", ...)` is a color, not SGR faint. TUI lines terminate with SGR/OSC-8 reset. **Recommendation:** map semantic foreground roles through Pi tokens, but implement an explicit faint modifier if literal Codex faintness is required; do not claim a gray token is equivalent. [Color emission:102–131](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/theme/theme.js#L102-L131), [style methods:200–225](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/theme/theme.js#L200-L225), [line-reset contract:11–31](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/docs/tui.md#L11-L31).

With 256-color capability, Pi approximates RGB using channel cube `[0,95,135,175,215,255]`; grayscale competes only when RGB spread is below 10 and weighted squared distance is smaller (`0.299R²+0.587G²+0.114B²`). Thus the palette table is not a promise of identical rendered RGB on every terminal. [Color conversion:34–131](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/theme/theme.js#L34-L131).

Two helper exceptions matter for integration. `renderDiff` colors whole numbered lines with diff tokens; only exactly one removed line followed by exactly one added line receives word-diff **inverse** spans, excluding leading whitespace from the first changed part. It applies no dedicated added/removed background. Both `renderDiff` and installed `highlightCode(code, lang)` use Pi's global theme; despite a three-argument example in the extension docs, installed `highlightCode` has **two** parameters. The workspace already calls it with two. Highlighting falls back to `mdCodeBlock` when language support is unavailable, or raw lines if highlighting throws. [Intraline helper:24–61](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/diff.js#L24-L61), [diff dispatch:70–130](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/components/diff.js#L70-L130), [highlight helper:798–858](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/theme/theme.js#L798-L858).

### Extension-only architecture and remaining gaps

**Recommendation:** evolve the existing boundaries rather than add another tool-execution layer:

| Existing module              | Integration responsibility                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `builtins.ts` / owner opt-in | Preserve execution, schema, prompt metadata, settings, active list, and ownership.                                                   |
| `replay.ts`                  | Isolate the one version-sensitive render lookup wrapper; preserve reversible disposal.                                               |
| `presentation.ts`            | Convert supplied args/results into tool-family semantics; do not infer execution from text or fabricate missing exit/abort metadata. |
| `layout.ts`                  | Normalize, wrap, budget, prefix, and clamp terminal rows; never mutate backend results.                                              |
| `renderers.ts`               | Project current context into self-shell components; keep per-slot caches and regenerate styles.                                      |

These boundaries already exist in code and its integration contract. No Pi core/dependency edits are needed for per-row presentation. [Workspace navigation/constraints:3–13](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/AGENTS.md#L3-L13), [renderer factory:232–249](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.ts#L232-L249).

**Grouped ownership is not implemented.** Codex owns ordered calls in one `ExecCell`, routes completion by ID, and can retain a completed exploration beside a running sibling; committed assertions explicitly verify this. Pi instead allocates one host component per call, with independent images, click expansion, and saved definition. Empty sibling components can remove text rows, but cannot transfer their image ownership or expansion/replay state to an anchor through `ToolRenderContext`. A shared event observer alone does not solve this, since replay does not reproduce those events. Faithful grouping would require a separately scoped extension-level transcript owner and explicit host coordination; it is not a renderer-only change within this package. [Codex group ownership](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L89-L145), [Codex overlapping-call assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L726-L755), [Pi per-call allocation](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L2627-L2645).

### Projection boundaries and HTML export

Codex exposes distinct main-chat, raw, and transcript methods; Pi's tool callbacks expose one expansion boolean. This workspace has no raw projection or transcript pager. HTML export is outside the package's declared scope and has a separate rendering dispatcher. [Codex projection contract](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L186-L250).

Session `/export` excludes the exact names `"bash"`, `"read"`, `"write"`, `"edit"`, and `"ls"` from TUI pre-rendering through `TEMPLATE_RENDERED_TOOLS`. This is a **name-based exclusion**, including extensions overriding those names. Their template branches ignore this package's TUI renderer overrides. [Export dispatch:118–158](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/export-html/index.js#L118-L158).

| Export route                                       | Actual projection                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Session `bash`                                     | `"$ "` plus command; trimmed result text with a five-line expandable preview; no `"◦ Running"` heading from this package                                                |
| Session `write`                                    | Submitted `args.content` is rendered independently of the TUI call slot, with a ten-line preview and expandable full content when longer; final result text is separate |
| Session `read`, `edit`, `ls`                       | Dedicated template branches; respectively a ten-line text preview with images, complete `details.diff` when present, and a twenty-line listing preview                  |
| Session `powershell`, `grep`, `find`, custom names | Eligible for the TUI adapter when the registered definition supplies renderers; otherwise generic template fallback                                                     |
| Standalone CLI `exportFromFile()`                  | Constructs session data without `renderedTools` or a renderer adapter; uses template branches and generic fallback                                                      |

[Template branches:914–1065](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/export-html/template.js#L914-L1065), [standalone export:199–225](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/export-html/index.js#L199-L225).

For calls that reach the adapter, it renders at width **100**, with `P=true/E=true/A=true/X=false` for the call, then renders collapsed/expanded final results without rerendering the call. A decorated completed **`powershell`** can therefore retain this package's `"◦ Running"` header. This synthetic pending state does not describe ordinary `bash` export. The self-shell host spacer is not involved in this adapter. Export lookup calls `getToolDefinition()` directly, whereas TUI lookup additionally merges missing slots through `withBuiltInRenderers()`; export does not inherit that TUI fallback merge. [Adapter:27–105](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/export-html/tool-renderer.js#L27-L105), [export lookup:2755–2767](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/agent-session.js#L2755-L2767), [TUI lookup:1545–1551](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/modes/interactive/interactive-mode.js#L1545-L1551), [per-slot inheritance:35–45](/home/sus/.vite-plus/js_runtime/node/24.19.0/lib/node_modules/@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent/dist/core/tools/renderers/index.js#L35-L45).

Existing assertions cover partial/final replacement, concurrent row isolation, width clamping, error visibility, numbered diffs, metadata/active-list preservation, two reloads, unload restoration, and wrapper disposal. They were inspected, not executed in this research. Most renderer tests use identity theme functions and therefore do not prove actual default colors; the palette and span claims above come from implementation. Terminal image protocols, mouse behavior, rendered HTML, and runtime behavior after a Pi upgrade remain unverified interactively. [Renderer assertions:6–142](../../packages/extensions/pi-tool-display/src/renderers.test.ts#L6-L142), [registration assertions:47–134](#historical-workspace-sources), [replay assertions:55–204](#historical-workspace-sources).

## Integration conformance

These are **expected behaviors for a future pi implementation**, not tests executed by this report. Keep source-compatible behavior separate from deliberate pi-specific differences. A plain-text snapshot cannot verify a foreground, background, DIM, BOLD, or strike-through modifier; style assertions need span/ANSI inspection as well as visible text.

| Case                                                                         | Required observation for source parity                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Agent command pending → exit 0 → nonzero variant                             | Pending activity marker, then green/bold or red/bold `•`; `Running` becomes `Ran`. No generic elapsed footer in compact chat.                                                                                                                                  | [Exec state tests](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L546-L599)                                                                                                                                                          |
| Output arrives before completion                                             | Call remains active despite an output object. Authoritative completion replaces live output rather than appending it again.                                                                                                                                    | [Exec model](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/model.rs#L115-L203)                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Read A, read B, repeated read A                                              | One exploration group; contiguous all-read calls combine names in first-seen order without duplicates. Original calls remain separately inspectable.                                                                                                           | [Exploration rendering](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L255-L350)                                                                                                                                                               |
| Overlapping exploration: first call fails while sibling runs                 | Group stays active until all calls finish; then compact text is still `Explored`, with failure details in transcript. A pi error summary here is a documented divergence.                                                                                      | [Failure regression](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L122-L160)                                                                                                                                                        |
| Missing begin beside unrelated running exploration                           | Separate finalized history entry; no attachment to or premature commit of the unrelated active group.                                                                                                                                                          | [Orphan regression](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L644-L685)                                                                                                                                                         |
| Same read-like command executed by the user                                  | `You ran`, ordinary output display, 50-row budget; never silently grouped as exploration.                                                                                                                                                                      | [User-shell regression](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L1252-L1278)                                                                                                                                                   |
| Long command vs long output                                                  | Command continuation ellipsis omits transcript hint; output ellipsis includes it. Command and output budgets are independent.                                                                                                                                  | [Command omission test](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L996-L1016), [output omission test](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L846-L877)        |
| One huge URL, many empty lines, or narrow width                              | Count physical rows after wrapping; preserve documented adaptive URL behavior; never equate grapheme count with columns. If pi enforces per-string width, explicitly wrap the Codex oversized logical line.                                                    | [URL/height regressions](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L1032-L1192)                                                                                                                                                            |
| Live output crosses 1 MiB, split CRLF, huge ANSI sequence, Unicode head/tail | Retention bounds hold independently of preview height; newline accounting and retained suffix stay valid. Expanded output labels retained omissions honestly.                                                                                                  | [Live-output tests](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/live_output_tests.rs#L9-L133)                                                                                                                                                          |
| MCP invocation barely fits vs exceeds remaining header columns               | Inline `Called invocation`, otherwise standalone `Called` and invocation under `"  └ "`; following result does not receive a second branch marker. Use unadjusted `Line::width()` for this fit test, not Codex's corrected wrapping width.                     | [MCP layout](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L162-L285)                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Standard MCP with many content blocks                                        | Per-block formatting; no global five-row cap and no guarantee that transcript is untruncated. Distinguish error text from result `is_error`.                                                                                                                   | [MCP projection tests](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_tests.rs#L126-L180)                                                                                                                                                          |
| Valid image vs malformed/partial image                                       | Successfully parsed image blocks become summaries; the separate image-output notice requires complete decoding. A malformed block instead falls back to serialized JSON, which can include base64; suppressing that fallback in pi is a deliberate divergence. | [Image projection test](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp_tests.rs#L182-L197)                                                                                                                                                         |
| Node REPL success with empty envelope output                                 | Keep title/header but suppress recognized empty successful output; transcript restores invocation and original top-level text. Standard MCP must not acquire this special policy.                                                                              | [REPL branch](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L162-L247)                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Patch start, successful completion, failed completion, declined completion   | Start emits diff summary. Success adds nothing; only `Failed` adds failure cell in this handler. No generic green completion transition and no invented declined failure block.                                                                                | [Patch lifecycle](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tool_lifecycle.rs#L154-L165)                                                                                                                                                            |
| Patch under light/dark, truecolor/ANSI256/ANSI16                             | Assert actual per-span colors, line backgrounds, line-number/sign style, and capability fallbacks; do not validate only `+`/`-` text.                                                                                                                          | [Diff renderer and tests](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/diff_render.rs)                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Reduced motion and pending MCP transcript                                    | Static dim marker with animations disabled; otherwise MCP live-tail cache receives 50 ms ticks. Exec transcript has no equivalent animated bullet.                                                                                                             | [MCP tick](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L315-L321), [exec transcript](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/exec_cell/render.rs#L195-L239)                           |
| Default desired height exceeds 65,535 rows                                   | Returned logical lines remain intact, but `try_into().unwrap_or(0)` reports height **0**. A pi clamp to 65,535 or unlimited height differs from this source contract.                                                                                          | [Height conversion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L223-L264)                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Multiline raw MCP transport error                                            | `"alpha\nbeta"` becomes one line with visible `"Error: alphabeta"`; do not substitute rich-error wrapping or persisted-export splitting.                                                                                                                       | [Raw constructor](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mcp.rs#L288-L312), [locked string-to-line conversion](https://github.com/ratatui/ratatui/blob/e665c36cb14752a61cd777fbd06dbef8474f2add/ratatui-core/src/text/line.rs#L222-L258)       |
| Light diff in finalized scrollback versus transcript                         | Native writer's line style overrides the gutter background; transcript span style preserves it. ANSI16 has no changed-line background.                                                                                                                         | [Scrollback composition](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/insert_history.rs#L293-L340), [transcript adapter](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/terminal_hyperlinks/paragraph.rs#L21-L47) |
| Resize/theme change or shrinking live output                                 | Recompute width/theme-dependent spans and heights; remove old glyphs; no stale cached theme colors.                                                                                                                                                            | [Layout cache](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/transcript.rs#L8-L101), [active paint](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/history_cell/mod.rs#L288-L313)                       |
| Transcript open while calls stream and finish                                | Changed tail recomputes once; committed cell is not duplicated; user-scrolled position remains stable.                                                                                                                                                         | [Pager tail](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/pager_overlay.rs#L768-L803)                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Session replay includes success/failure/declined commands                    | Recreate call identity, output, normalized exit, and duration, not pre-rendered ANSI rows.                                                                                                                                                                     | [Replay assertion](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/121f91fd5d9dc66017866ce9bdc49f1e182721df/codex-rs/tui/src/chatwidget/tests/exec_flow.rs#L162-L250)                                                                                                                                                          |

The integration chapter identifies which of these behaviors the current workspace already supports and which need changes. This revision does not change tool execution, model-visible results, permissions, or any pi/Codex source.
